Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [101] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法，通过预测干净token的后验均值并利用精确的核依赖线性映射转换为得分，解决了离散扩散模型中得分比率违反贝叶斯可实现性的问题。M2S在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText上均显著优于现有纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) 的参数化虽然保证了正向性，但无法确保得分比率满足贝叶斯可实现性（即噪声状态下的得分比率不能由任何干净token的后验分布联合诱导），导致大量得分向量无效，产生负的预归一化权重并损害生成质量。

Method: 提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法：先预测干净token的后验均值，再通过精确的核依赖线性映射将其转换为得分。该方法适用于任何满足弱支持条件的坐标式连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。对于均匀腐蚀，M2S将概率单纯形映射到桥多面体；对于吸收掩码腐蚀，其目标函数精确恢复MD4。

Result: 1) 将原始得分投影到桥多面体上消除了所有负权重，并将生成PPL从203.6降至175.1。2) 在CIFAR-10上（28.4M参数），M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著降低。3) 在OpenWebText上（170M参数，~262B tokens），M2S在每种采样预算下均优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC，128步时生成PPL达143.3 vs 基线最强183.6。

Conclusion: M2S通过确保得分的贝叶斯可实现性，有效消除了负权重问题并显著提升离散扩散模型的生成质量，方法简洁且在多种设定下均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [102] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“JITAI-Twins”的数字孪生方法，用于在即时自适应干预（JITAI）部署前模拟目标子人群，以评估候选在线学习算法。该方法基于条件时间序列扩散模型，通过三步信息更新（预训练在大规模观察数据、微调在相关人群的小型干预部署、推理时校准领域专家知识）生成时间一致的数字孪生体。在HeartSteps系列（v2至v4）研究中验证，该方法能比简单模拟器更好地复现目标人群的时间和参与者间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中使用的在线学习算法若设计不当，可能会给参与者带来负担并导致其脱离干预。因此，在每次实际部署前，需要先通过逼真的模拟用户对新算法设计进行验证，避免不良后果。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型构建数字孪生体，确保时间一致性（未来动作不影响已生成的过去）。采用三步信息更新：1）在大规模观察数据集上预训练；2）在相关人群的先前干预部署数据上微调；3）通过领域科学家专业知识在推理时校准到目标人群。

Result: 在HeartSteps系列（v2至v4）的实际部署验证中，所提方法在复现目标子人群的时间结构和参与者间结构方面优于更简单的模拟器。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生体可在实际部署前模拟目标干预场景，为在线算法设计决策的测试和优化提供前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [103] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于局部上下文加权的离散流匹配方法，通过改进连续时间马尔可夫链的采样过程和对数损失函数的重新加权，显著提升了离散生成模型的质量与效率，在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低高达63%，并能在任意顺序生成中匹配半自回归块扩散基线的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的因子化训练目标使模型同时面对难易程度不同的预测目标（从低熵的确定性标记到高熵的模糊标记），导致训练信号不均匀。作者观察到每个标记的不确定性与其邻域上下文的密度密切相关，因此希望利用局部上下文信息来改进训练和采样过程。

Method: 1) 提出上下文加权采样器（context-weighted sampler），对底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）进行简单修改以纳入局部上下文信息；2) 提出缩放交叉熵损失函数（scaled cross-entropy loss），对不同标记的训练信号重新加权；3) 在训练和采样两个阶段同时引入局部上下文感知机制。

Result: 1) 在OpenWebText上生成困惑度降低高达63%；2) 在生成质量上匹配强半自回归块扩散基线；3) 保留任意顺序生成的能力；4) 计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的一个重要因素，简单的上下文感知修改就能显著提升采样和训练效率。该方法在不需要半自回归约束的前提下达到了接近块扩散方法的生成质量，展示了离散流匹配框架中利用局部信息的潜力。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant提出了一种基于Kronecker结构的可逆变换用于扩散Transformer的W4A4后训练量化，在32元素块上应用，参数少于逐通道缩放，且推理速度比SmoothQuant快14%，在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell等模型上取得了优于SVDQuant和LoRaQ的量化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在W4A4量化时，激活值中的异常值导致4-bit格式无法准确表示，严重降低输出质量。现有方法在量化精度与推理开销之间存在权衡：SmoothQuant计算廉价但量化精度差；固定Hadamard变换需大块大小导致高在线成本；学习的全维度可逆变换虽校准最优但每层每步需密集矩阵乘法。

Method: KroQuant对每个32元素激活块应用学习的Kronecker结构可逆变换，以块局部结构执行小型张量核心GEMM；结合离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收逐权重的残余量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器内核比SmoothQuant内核快14%；在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell的W4A4（MXFP4e2）设置下，KroQuant在MJHQ-30K和SDCI上比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考输出，且保持或提升了图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构可逆变换在量化精度与推理效率之间取得了更好的平衡，参数少于逐通道缩放，同时推理速度快于SmoothQuant，量化质量优于现有方法。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [105] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTEL（测试时扩展通过错误定位）算法，利用环境反馈进行token级错误定位，通过比较条件概率识别推理轨迹中的错误步骤，截断后复用有效前缀重新生成。在代码和数学推理任务上，TTEL以更少的token生成量建立了严格的帕累托最优前沿，相比独立采样等方法显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法（如独立采样、多轮精炼）缺乏token级信用分配，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，计算效率低下。本文旨在通过细粒度的错误定位来提升推理时计算效率。

Method: TTEL算法利用固定或环境反馈进行token级错误定位，通过比较有信息反馈与无上下文基线下的条件概率，隔离出推理轨迹中发生错误的步骤，然后截断该步骤并分支生成新内容，最大程度复用有效前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，Qwen3-8B使用TTEL达到71.0%的pass@64，生成token量仅为独立采样的约一半（360.4k vs 735.0k）。在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均显著优于其他测试时基线方法。

Conclusion: TTEL通过token级错误定位和前缀复用，在序列推理任务上建立了严格的帕累托最优前沿，以更少的计算成本实现了更强或相当的推理性能，证明了细粒度信用分配对提升推理时计算效率的重要性。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [106] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 证明确定性KV缓存驱逐会导致注意力误差无法被一致估计，而随机化驱逐（泊松采样+ Hájek校正）可恢复可识别性，并提供具有高经验覆盖率的逐步误差证书。实验验证了七个预注册声明中的四个，发现证书在归因而非预测方面更有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性KV缓存驱逐虽然保留top-k重要token，但无法知晓被移除的内容，导致注意力误差估计器不一致。需要一种可识别、可认证的驱逐策略。

Method: 提出随机化驱逐策略：对尾部token进行泊松采样（已知包含概率），在softmax中使用Hájek校正（单个logit偏移），并基于调查抽样方差估计构造逐步误差证书。

Result: 随机化策略的误差证书经验覆盖率达0.97且无准确率损失。证书在分离缓存引入误差与模型固有误差上AUC 0.73-0.75（输出置信度仅0.47-0.54），并优于随机/置信度门控的重计算调度。

Conclusion: 随机化带来的是归因能力（attribution），而非预测能力（prediction）。证书不能直接预测失败，但能有效归因失败来源并指导重计算决策。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [107] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出主-代理原型规划系统(MAPS)，一种分层深度强化学习架构，通过集中式主代理生成全局协调策略的紧凑连续嵌入(原型规划)，分散式工人代理结合局部观察执行车辆控制，在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航并显著降低平均行驶时间，且展现出良好的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无信号交叉口中自动驾驶车辆协调的挑战，现有MARL系统在组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或僵化代理设计方面存在困难。

Method: 提出MAPS分层深度强化学习架构：集中式Master代理生成紧凑连续嵌入(proto-plan)编码全局协调策略，分散式Worker代理结合该嵌入与局部观察执行车辆控制，实现策略意图与战术执行的解耦及各模块独立优化。

Result: 在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航并显著降低平均旅行时间，优于现有基线；训练于三代理场景的系统零样本部署至五代理场景时达到94%成功率。

Conclusion: 基于原型规划的分层学习为多车辆协调提供了有前景的框架，proto-plan机制具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [108] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Windowed-MTP，对MTP草案模型的自注意力施加滑动窗口+注意力汇聚，将其KV工作集限制为常数，在百万级上下文下以训练无关、即插即用、无损的方式将草案KV读取降低约99%，在Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B和Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B上每解码步成本降低28%-44%，并回收未读取的草案KV内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中，MTP草案头在每个草案步对完整KV缓存执行全注意力，导致其读取成本随上下文线性增长，在百万级上下文下草案成本主导解码过程，削弱甚至抵消推测解码的加速收益；这一问题在混合/线性注意力目标模型下更为严重，因为更廉价的验证使草案的全注意力读取成本更加突出。

Method: 对MTP草案的注意力模块应用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口加注意力汇聚（attention sink），将草案的KV工作集限制为固定窗口大小；保留目标模型的全注意力验证不变，因此窗口化仅改变提案内容，不影响最终接受的token分布，保证无损；使用紧凑环形缓冲区回收未读取的草案KV内存。

Result: 在三个架构家族（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B、Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）上，1M上下文单GPU环境下，窗口化将每解码步成本降低28%-44%；丢弃约99%的KV条目；在匹配接受率下端到端解码延迟同比改善；回收了7.7%-11%的总KV内存。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP是一种训练无关、即插即用、无损的草案注意力优化方法，有效解决了长上下文下MTP草案的注意力读取成本问题，可广泛适用于现有带MTP草案的模型。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [109] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架，通过文本教师模型向音频语言学生模型迁移推理能力，并构建三层对称语料库（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理），显著提升音频基础推理与思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在深度逻辑推理方面落后于文本大语言模型，主要原因是缺乏高质量音频推理数据。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架：学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；同时构建三层对称语料库（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理），将跨模态蒸馏扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话语境中的推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR基准上，X$^3$-OPD显著提升了音频基础推理和思维链质量，同时在域转移下较好地保留了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态同策略蒸馏结合三层对称语料库能有效弥合音频语言模型与文本语言模型在逻辑推理能力上的差距。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [110] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EnsembleEGNN，一种分子构象集成基础模型，通过共享EGNN层编码每个构象体，再用集合注意力池化形成整体表示。在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集上，从头训练失败(R²=0.005)，但预训练后达到R²=0.477，优于序列BERT基线；与BERT联合训练进一步提升至R²=0.538。证明编码构象集成可改善环肽性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测通常只使用单个代表性构象，忽略了许多分子在溶液中以构象集成形式存在的事实，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN：1) 用共享EGNN层独立编码每个构象体；2) 用集合注意力块(Set Attention Block)池化所有构象表示；3) 在CREMP环肽构象集成数据集上进行多任务自监督预训练，包括掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重建和成对距离重建。

Result: 在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB上：从头训练R²=0.005(几乎失效)；预训练后R²=0.477, Pearson r=0.699，优于BERT基线(R²=0.439, r=0.667)；与BERT联合端到端训练达R²=0.538, r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集成编码为单一热力学信息嵌入可显著改善环肽性质预测，且与序列编码器联合训练效果更优。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS是一个两阶段必要性感知框架，通过反事实干预识别时间序列分类器中既充分又必要的关键子序列，在合成和真实数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列解释方法仅关注充分性（即找到足以维持预测的子序列），可能将高分赋予虚假的、支持预测但非决策必需的子序列，缺乏对必要性的考量。

Method: 采用两阶段设计：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程和充分性导向的解释掩码；Stage II对时间因子进行反事实干预以获取必要性信号，通过时序门控机制细化初始解释，抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要的成分。

Result: 在合成和真实时间序列基准上，TimePNS更准确地识别决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合Pearl的反事实必要性概念，通过干预方式评估时间因子的必要性，可以有效过滤充分但不必要的虚假子序列，提升时间序列解释的精度。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows)和Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)，一种可在连续和离散空间上生成可变维度/长度输出的流模型框架。通过引入扩展插值因子（expand interpolant）逐步增广状态空间，并设计扩展算子与传输算子的组合，实现从固定尺寸流模型到可变尺寸生成模型的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型（如连续流、离散流）受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出尺寸本身作为自由度的场景（如图形节点数可变、序列长度可变）。本文旨在建立一种原则上可处理可变输出大小的流模型框架。

Method: 1. 定义Expanding Generative Flows：在流过程中引入扩展插值因子，逐步增广状态空间（增加新坐标或token），且新增部分以当前状态为条件。2. 提出Expanding Flow Maps：将任意两个时间步之间的映射分解为两个可学习操作——扩展算子（expand operator）增广状态空间，传输算子（transport map）沿插值因子推演状态。两者复合得到同时扩展与去噪的单一映射。3. 将框架推广到离散单纯形，支持变大小图生成和变长序列生成。

Result: 在连续和离散两种模态上，EFlows和EFMs被验证为一种原则性框架，可处理输出尺寸本身作为可学习、可控自由度的场景。当扩展算子退化为恒等映射时，该方法退化为现有的固定画布流和流映射。

Conclusion: EFlows/EFMs提供了一种统一、可扩展的方法，使基于流的生成模型具备处理可变输出尺寸的能力，拓展了流模型在图形生成、序列生成等需要动态输出大小的任务中的应用范围。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AINTMA，一种基于六类专用AI代理的智能测试管理架构，通过强化学习、生成式智能和零信任安全通信，在12个软件项目、18个月的评估中实现88.4%的测试优先级排序准确率、43%的测试周期缩减和340%的投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保证需要能够在分布式云环境中进行自适应决策的智能自主系统，传统测试管理方法已无法满足大规模、多云环境下的质量保障需求。

Method: 设计六类专用AI代理（测试发现、风险评估、RL优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能、云安全监控），通过零信任API网关和OAuth2/JWT认证的安全多代理通信框架协调，在云原生微服务基础设施上运行。RL代理将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用47维特征和36个月滚动窗口学习策略。

Result: 88.4%测试优先级排序准确率（APFD，对比随机51.2%、最佳商业基线82.1%）；43%测试周期缩减；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；340% ROI，9个月回本；可扩展至50,000+测试用例且响应时间低于400ms；生成式质量智能模块获得4.3/5.0开发者有用性评分。

Conclusion: 结合自主多代理协调、生成式智能和安全智能连接的代理型AI可以根本性地推进云规模企业环境中的软件质量管理。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [114] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大语言模型水印对医学领域性能的影响，发现水印会导致词法错误、术语幻觉、图像发现误归因/遗漏等多种临床关键性退化，而现有通用基准无法捕捉这些失败模式，因此必须进行领域特定的评估才能安全部署水印模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地应用于临床工作流，需要可靠的可追溯性（水印），但现有水印评估集中在通用基准，忽视了医学领域中小词级别扰动可能引发重大语义变化的问题。

Method: 在11个LLM和7个VLM上基准测试5种水印方案，涵盖单模态和多模态临床推理任务；引入经人类专家验证的审计流程，系统评估医学推理质量、术语精确性和诱导的幻觉。

Result: 水印会导致词法错误、幻觉术语、图像发现误归因/遗漏等多种退化；缺乏领域特定分析以及聚合指标无法捕捉临床文本固有失败模式，会系统性地掩盖水印引起的实际退化。

Conclusion: 领域特定评估是安全部署水印模型于医学领域的先决条件，当前基准可能掩盖临床关键的失败模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [115] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个AI驱动的浏览器扩展，利用混合机器学习架构（Transformer嵌入+语言学特征+自定义"诱饵度"评分）检测点击诱饵文章，在开放数据集上达到91%的F1分数。该工具可在用户访问前后发出警告，提供点击诱饵概率百分比、基于多维度指标的解释，以及文章的一两句摘要（剧透）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的点击诱饵检测方法存在局限，用户缺乏有效的实时防护工具来避免被误导性的网络文章欺骗。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构：结合基于Transformer的嵌入表示、语言学启发的特征以及自定义的"baitness"评分。从经典向量化方法到LLM嵌入等多种NLP技术中评估，最终开发了基于XGBoost的模型。

Result: 在开放组合数据集上达到91%的F1分数；浏览器扩展可在用户访问点击诱饵前后发出警告，显示概率百分比，提供基于分析指标的解释，并生成文章的1-2句摘要作为"剧透"。

Conclusion: 该工具不仅实现了高精度的点击诱饵检测，还通过实时警告、可解释性评分和内容摘要功能，为用户提供了全面的防护体验。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [116] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了单模型自洽性（重复运行100次）与多样本集成（24个不同LLM各运行一次）在揭示模型未知知识方面的能力。通过Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验，发现单模型自洽性在跨问题结构上几乎无信号（最多一个维度显著），而多样本集成则能检测到多个显著维度，表明自洽性适用于逐问题不确定性估计，但只有多样本集成能揭示模型间的协同知识结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型重复运行时的变异性是否不仅能用于逐问题的不确定性估计（自洽性），还能揭示跨问题之间的结构相关性（类似多样本集成的协同效应）。

Method: 在相同问题上比较两种设置：（1）单个模型重复运行100次（温度τ=1）；（2）24个不同LLM组成的集成各运行一次（τ=0）。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验分离信号与采样噪声，并在五个模型家族、三个基准（MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K）上进行验证。

Result: 单模型自洽性最多只有一个维度高于噪声水平；而多样本集成有四个特征值显著超过噪声阈值。匹配难度的伯努利零模型在500次蒙特卡罗模拟中最多产生一个显著维度。

Conclusion: 自洽性提供准确的逐问题不确定性估计，但无法检测到跨问题结构；只有多样本集成才能揭示模型所不知道的知识结构。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [117] [ConfidenceBench: Evaluating Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20526)
*Matthew ffrench-Constant,Daniel Yang,Xinmeng Huang,Sanyam Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConfidenceBench校准基准，使用Brier分数评估15个前沿LLM的言语化置信度估计。测试发现Claude Opus 4.6和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview校准最佳（Brier分数0.103），而准确率最高的模型并非校准最佳，部分模型尽管准确率尚可但校准差于随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估仅关注准确率，忽略了模型在不确定时应知道自身局限性的能力。在代价高昂的应用场景中，模型需要能够表达可靠的置信度，而不仅仅是追求答案正确。

Method: 构建包含200道私有多项选择题的ConfidenceBench基准，涵盖空间推理、高精度数学、单词查找和不可知问题四类。通过提示词诱导模型输出言语化置信度，不依赖logits，使用Brier分数作为适当评分规则进行评估。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview取得最低Brier分数（0.103），优于校准随机基线（0.1875）；Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite严重失准（0.367）。准确率与校准在不同模型族中存在显著分歧，最准确的模型并非校准最佳。

Conclusion: 言语化置信度校准是LLM可靠性的一个独立且重要的维度，与传统准确率评估互补。模型具备较高准确率并不意味着良好的校准能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where fluent but incorrect answers can be costly. In these settings, accuracy alone is insufficient: models must also know when they are likely to be wrong. We present ConfidenceBench, a calibration benchmark that evaluates verbalized confidence estimates in 15 frontier LLMs using the Brier score, a proper scoring rule that incentivises truthful probability reporting. Confidence is elicited via prompting, requiring no access to model logits and making the framework applicable to both closed-source and open-source systems. The benchmark comprises 200 private multiple-choice questions across four categories: spatial reasoning, high-precision mathematics, word lookup, and unknowable questions. Across three independent runs, Claude Opus 4.6 and Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview achieve the lowest Brier scores, both reported as 0.103. Both substantially outperform the calibrated-random baseline of 0.1875, while Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite scores 0.367, indicating severe miscalibration. Accuracy and calibration diverge substantially across model families: the most accurate model is not the best-calibrated, and several models perform worse than the calibrated-random Brier baseline despite reasonable accuracy. These results show that verbalized confidence calibration is a distinct and practically important axis of LLM reliability, complementary to standard accuracy-based evaluation.

</details>


### [118] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAXBench是一个面向Google Cloud TPU的50个JAX工作负载的基准测试套件，用于AI生成内核优化。包含17个生产级ML算子（来自Llama-3.1、DeepSeek-V3等架构）和33个KernelBench转换算子。实验表明，在稀疏文档的DSL（Pallas）中，目标特定上下文比模型规模更重要——条件化TPU文档将正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，解决48/50个基准，几何平均加速1.28倍；Autocomp波束搜索达1.36倍几何平均加速，在手调内核上达1.60倍（接近Tokamax的2.08倍上界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核性能优化有严格基准推动进步，但TPU缺乏类似基准，因此需要为TPU建立原生基准套件以支持AI生成内核优化研究。

Method: 构建含50个JAX workloads的基准套件，其中17个从MaxText库中提取生产级ML算子并配以Tokamax手调Pallas内核作为专家基线，33个从KernelBench转换并设置新问题大小以达到高TPU v6e MXU利用率。评估四种反馈驱动方法生成候选Pallas内核，对比有无TPU文档条件化的效果及不同搜索策略。

Result: 条件化TPU文档将正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，解决48/50个基准，几何平均加速1.28倍；Autocomp波束搜索达1.36倍几何平均加速；在手调内核上达1.60倍（Tokamax上界2.08倍），但在分页和稀疏注意力算子上仍有差距。

Conclusion: 高质量TPU内核优化仍具挑战性，已开源JAXBench基准、评估工具和基线结果以支持社区贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [119] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap是一个无需训练的框架，通过动态连续验证策略解决扩散大语言模型中的联合概率依赖误差（JPDE），在中等置信度下实现可靠加速，在MBPP上最高可达53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache时可达105.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的并行解码策略因过于保守的置信度阈值导致冗余去噪迭代和次优推理速度，其根本原因是联合概率依赖误差（JPDE）的存在。

Method: 提出DC-Leap框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 动态连续验证策略（Dynamic Contiguous Verification），将严格有序的因果约束整合到并行解码中，逐步验证token依赖关系，消除JPDE；(2) 草稿引导解码机制（draft-guided decoding），利用草稿向前跳跃多个token扩展上下文，在推理时保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，DC-Leap在MBPP长序列生成任务上实现了最高53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache后加速比达到105.02倍，同时保持可比的生成质量。

Conclusion: DC-Leap通过消除JPDE实现了dLLMs在中等置信度下的可靠加速，是一种有效的训练无关加速框架。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [120] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench是一个评估AI代理在LLM推理优化任务中能力的基准测试。代理需要在两小时内利用H100 GPU优化LLM推理速度，涵盖预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发吞吐量及综合平衡四种场景。15个前沿代理虽能显著超越朴素PyTorch基线（最高8.08倍），但低于简单超参数搜索（最高11.53倍）。分析发现代理倾向于收敛到单一推理框架，缺乏探索多样性策略的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常评估代理在预设工作流或狭窄动作空间上的表现，即使是名义上开放的任务也常可通过检索已知配方和调参解决，难以区分结果是真正优化还是记忆性解决方案。因此需要设计一个能反映代理在开放式AI工程环境中真实能力的基准。

Method: 设计InferenceBench基准，要求代理部署兼容OpenAI的推理服务器并优化LLM推理速度。每个代理获得一个目标LLM、一块H100 GPU、一个优化场景和两小时时间预算。包含预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发吞吐量和三者平衡共四种优化场景。在15个前沿代理配置上进行评估，并进行轨迹定性分析。

Result: 代理能可靠地超越朴素PyTorch基线（最高8.08倍），通常能匹配或超过默认设置的vLLM（4.05倍），但低于相同时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高11.53倍）。轨迹分析显示代理列举了大量优化技术，但绝大多数收敛于单一推理框架，只测试少量不同配置，剩余时间用于重测、修复或优化超参数，而非探索不同策略。

Conclusion: 代理的瓶颈不在于领域知识，而在于提出多样化配置、系统评估并提交最佳方案的能力。InferenceBench反映了代理在开放式AI工程环境中的真实能力，其中记忆性解决方案只能带来有限的改进。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [121] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DecodeShare协议，识别LLM在解码时（而非预填充时）跨任务共享的低维子空间，并通过干预实验证明该子空间对决策性能有因果影响，且可用于激活引导等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM使用同一组参数处理多种任务，但在KV缓存推理模式下，解码阶段是否存在任务通用的结构尚不清楚。现有研究多关注预填充阶段的表示，而解码阶段的通用表征及其因果作用未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议：首先识别解码时隐藏状态中跨任务一致共享的低维子空间，然后通过移除该子空间（仅解码阶段）进行因果干预实验，比较其对决策性能的影响与预填充子空间和随机子空间的差异。

Result: 干扰该共享子空间导致的决策性能下降远大于在相同干预预算下干扰预填充子空间或随机子空间。该解码共享子空间还与激活引导中的常见引导方向重叠，投影去除该子空间可分离两成分的功能角色，且解码时评估引导向量比预填充代理更可靠。

Conclusion: 尽管该共享子空间紧凑，但它在解码时充当了一个高杠杆的因果通道，对理解和优化LLM解码行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [122] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE是一个基于规划的抽取式LLM构建框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理三个模块，并引入DTI规划器选择最优基础模型及其组合，以降低标注成本并提升特定任务的构建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模指令微调数据集标注成本高，且缺乏针对特定任务优化LLM的方法。

Method: 提出PlanE框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理；并引入Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI)规划器，用于选择最优的基础LLM及其DTI组合。

Result: 实验从两个视角验证了有效性：(1) 相同基础LLM在不同数据集上；(2) 相同数据集在不同基础LLM上。此外，DTI规划器在不同优化目标下具有泛化性。

Conclusion: PlanE能有效提升抽取式LLM的构建效率，降低标注成本，且DTI规划器具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [123] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有大语言模型个性化基准仅测量发送方适应（服务于同一用户），缺乏对两方博弈（生成消息是否诱导第三方采取预期行动）的可复现评估。本文基于贝叶斯劝导理论，构建了销售场景下的生成式个性化框架，发布了包含6,279条客户成功案例的SDR-Bench数据集，通过时间约束模拟防止数据泄露。实验发现前沿模型存在个性化瓶颈，在财富100强科技队列中无法区分成功与不成功的沟通。12名专业销售代表的现场部署验证了框架有效性（48%内容即时可用，高级专家一致性Pearson 0.82）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化基准仅测量发送方适应（模型服务同一用户时的表现），而经典两方问题——生成消息是否诱导第三方采取预期行动——仅通过A/B测试和小规模人工研究进行，无法在需求时针对新模型复现。

Method: 将Kamenica和Gentzkow（2011）的贝叶斯劝导框架适配到生成式智能体，在销售场景中实例化（接收方行动与其触达消息常规记录）。构建SDR-Bench数据集（6,279条客户成功故事，22个行业，约200家企业），通过时间约束模拟防止未来数据泄露。

Result: 前沿LLM和深度研究智能体均存在一致的个性化瓶颈；在财富100强科技队列中，无模型能从统计上区分成功与不成功的沟通。现场部署48%模型生成内容评为即时有用，高级专家一致性Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 现有生成式模型在经典两方个性化任务中表现有限，已发布的SDR-Bench和SDR-Arena支持可复现的大规模生成式个性化研究。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [124] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种对话批评引导采样（DCGS）框架，通过推断每轮对话中的用户意图来增强LLM在多轮对抗性对话中的安全性，在多个基准上优于强鲁棒基线和前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全框架将多轮对话视为上下文无关的上下文赌博机问题，忽略了攻击者意图可能在多轮对话中逐步暴露的特性，导致要么误伤合法用户，要么容易被恶意利用。

Method: 将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在学习token级和utterance级的价值和遗憾批评器，通过动作-价值批评器对候选响应重新加权，该推理时重加权近似于对基础策略的指数倾斜，保证了有限候选池中期望回报的提升。

Result: 在CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench和Harmbench四个基准上，DCGS超越了强鲁棒基线和前沿模型；且无需微调即可迁移到前沿模型上提升其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过对话历史推断用户意图并动态调整回复，是提升多轮对抗性对话安全性的有效途径，且具有良好的迁移性。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [125] [Incomplete Prompt Jailbreaks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20473)
*Yeonjea Kim,Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了不完整提示越狱（IPJ）现象，系统研究了不完整有害提示如何诱导大语言模型生成有害续写，发现模型倾向于在句子终止后才拒绝，且参数调优难以泛化，最后通过识别终止神经元和续写神经元提出神经级干预的防御思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重的大语言模型虽设有安全护栏，但句子补全任务仍易受到不完整有害提示的攻击，现有研究对此缺乏系统性的形式化与实证分析。

Method: 形式化定义不完整提示越狱（IPJ）；分析多种吸引子类型；通过实验刻画模型在不完整有害提示下的续写行为；识别终止神经元和续写神经元两类功能性神经元。

Result: 模型在句子终止前系统性地延迟拒绝行为；参数调优训练模型拒绝不完整有害提示无法泛化到不同内容域和吸引子类型；识别出两类功能性神经元可用于精细控制。

Conclusion: 神经级干预比参数调优更精确、更鲁棒，为防御IPJ提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly released as open-weight models with safeguards against harmful requests. Nevertheless, sentence completion remains vulnerable to incomplete harmful prompts. In this work, we formalize this phenomenon as incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) and provide a systematic empirical characterization of when and how incomplete prompts elicit harmful continuations. We analyze diverse attractor types associated with incomplete sentence continuation and show that LLMs systematically delay refusal until sentence termination. We further demonstrate that training models to refuse incomplete harmful prompts via parameter tuning is insufficient, failing to generalize across both content domains and attractor types. To enable fine-grained control, we identify two functional neurons: termination and continuation neurons. By clarifying their roles in sentence completion, we highlight the potential of neuron-level interventions for more precise and robust IPJ defenses.

</details>


### [126] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriSimpl是一个基于简化验证的求解器LLM框架，通过生成简化的诊断查询让LLM推理优化建模的正确性，在多个基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言接口能极大提升优化建模的可及性和可用性，但现有方法难以确保LLM自动翻译的求解器公式化正确实现了预期任务（即使能无错误执行）。

Method: 提出简化验证思想：利用优化求解器生成简化的诊断查询，从约束和决策变量等多个维度进行简化，让LLM在固定的全局上下文中进行局部推理，从而判断公式化与任务描述的一致性。

Result: 在多个优化基准测试上，该方法在准确率上持续优于现有方法，同时提供了一种高精度的自验证信号。

Conclusion: 简化验证是提升LLM自动优化建模鲁棒性的有效途径，兼顾了准确性和可验证性。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can greatly benefit the accessibility and usability of optimization modeling, and recent advances in large language models (LLMs) show promise in automatically translating textual problem descriptions into executable solver formulations. However, a key challenge for existing approaches is to ensure that the inferred formulation correctly implements the intended task, even if it may execute without errors. We introduce VeriSimpl, a solver LLM framework for robust natural-language-to-optimization formalization. Our approach is based on the idea of simplification-based verification, where the optimization solver is leveraged to generate simplified diagnostic queries about a candidate formulation to allow the LLM to tractably reason about the correctness of the formulation with respect to the task description. We present such simplification strategies along different dimensions with respect to problem constraints and decision variables, which allow the LLM to reason locally under fixed global contexts. Evaluations on a range of optimization benchmarks show how our approach provides consistent improvements in accuracy over existing methods, while also providing a novel high-precision self-verification signal.

</details>


### [127] [Unsupervised Consensus-Based Anomaly Detection for Spatiotemporal Malaria Incidence in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21559)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文应用共识异常检测框架分析加纳2014-2023年月度疟疾监测数据，识别非典型传播模式。异常在时空上高度结构化，阿散蒂和北部地区是主要复发区域，塔马利、库马西和阿克拉为持续热点。关键发现是异常负担（异常期间累计病例数）与异常频率（持续异常行为）存在空间差异：塔马利异常负担最高，而异常频率最高的地区集中在阿散蒂各区。异常月份形成一个统计上显著的群体，其病例数（Cohen's d=3.252）和季节性偏差（d>1.2）远高于正常月份。疟疾负担本身无法完整反映传播动态，该框架通过区分疟疾高发区与传播行为异常区，可加强监测、优先调查并支持精准防控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疟疾监测仅关注病例负担（高发区域），但忽略了传播行为的异常模式（即非典型传播）。作者认为仅凭疟疾负担无法完整反映传播动态，需要通过区分"哪里疟疾最流行"与"哪里传播行为最异常"来强化监测体系，为精准防控提供依据。

Method: 采用共识异常检测框架（consensus anomaly detection framework），对加纳2014-2023年的月度疟疾监测数据进行分析，识别时空上非典型的传播模式，并量化异常负担与异常频率的空间分布差异。

Result: 异常在时空上高度结构化；阿散蒂和北部地区为最主要复发区域；塔马利、库马西和阿克拉为持续热点。异常负担（塔马利最高）与异常频率（阿散蒂各区最高）存在空间分离。异常月份的病例数（Cohen's d=3.252）和季节性偏差（d>1.2）显著高于正常月份。

Conclusion: 疟疾负担本身不足以完整描述传播动态。通过区分高负担区域与传播行为异常区域，该框架能够加强监测预警、优化调查优先级排序，并支持更具针对性的疟疾防控策略制定。

Abstract: A consensus anomaly detection framework was applied to monthly malaria surveillance data from Ghana (2014-2023) to identify atypical transmission patterns. Anomalies were highly structured in space and time. Ashanti and Northern Regions accounted for most recurrent anomalies, with persistent hotspots at Tamale, Kumasi, and Accra. A key finding was the spatial distinction between anomaly burden (cumulative cases during anomalous periods) and anomaly frequency (persistence of unusual behaviour). Tamale had the highest burden during anomalies, whereas the highest anomaly rates clustered in Ashanti districts, showing that high-burden areas are not necessarily those with the most frequent anomalous transmission. Anomalous months formed a statistically distinct group, with much higher case counts (Cohen's $d = 3.252$) and large seasonal deviations ($d > 1.2$) compared with normal months. Malaria burden alone provides an incomplete picture of transmission dynamics. By distinguishing where malaria is most prevalent from where transmission behaves most unusually, this framework can strengthen surveillance, prioritise investigations, and support targeted control strategies.

</details>


### [128] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler 提出了一套统一的 tile-aware Triton 内核，将 LLM 推理中的完整采样管线（logit 处理、token 选择、推测验证）垂直融合为固定的、工作负载感知的执行模型，支持动态逐请求采样行为并兼容 CUDA Graph，通过新颖的分层两阶段 top-k 算法实现了高达 10x-16x 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理采样实现要么只加速管线的子集，要么依赖多次内核启动，或假设批次内同质化采样行为，限制了动态服务工作负载的支持并阻碍了高效的 CUDA Graph 执行。

Method: 提出 SonicSampler，基于 tile-aware Triton 内核垂直融合完整采样管线；核心创新为分层两阶段 top-k 算法，利用 LLM 输出的低熵特性实现大词表上的高效选择；支持动态逐请求采样行为（包括语法约束解码、重复/频率/存在惩罚、logit 偏置、温度缩放、top-k/top-p/min-p 过滤以及推测验证）于单个批处理内核中，并完全兼容 CUDA Graph。

Result: 分层两阶段 top-k 算法相比竞争基线最高加速 10x；在异构推测解码工作负载下，相比最先进基线最高加速 16x。

Conclusion: SonicSampler 通过垂直融合采样管线并利用 LLM 输出低熵特性，在保持灵活批处理执行的同时实现了显著的加速效果，为动态服务工作负载提供了高效支持。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [129] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Multi-Sensor Physical Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20476)
*Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 五项大语言模型在多传感器物理安全数据评估中存在系统性缺陷：所有模型在单传感器超限场景中表现近乎完美，但在多传感器联合评估场景中几乎完全无法发出预警信号，结构化表格相比纯文本无一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大语言模型在多传感器物理危险数据评估中的实际表现能力，填补该领域系统性的实证评估空白。

Method: 设计60个场景（涵盖多传感器联合评估、响应相称性、模式消歧三类），使用5个LLM（ChatGPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、DeepSeek、Kimi、Llama 3.1 8B）在温度0.0下进行1800次API调用，比较结构化表格与纯文本格式的效果。

Result: 所有模型在多传感器联合场景（类别A）得分接近零（Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592），而在单传感器阈值违规场景（类别B Q1: 0.975-1.000）表现优异。结构化表格无一致优势，ChatGPT-4o在纯文本下表现显著更好（p=0.001）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM存在多传感器联合风险评估的严重盲区，可能在物理安全监控系统中产生漏报风险，对实际部署具有直接警示意义。

Abstract: We present an empirical benchmark evaluating how five large language models assess multisensor physical hazard data. Testing 60 scenarios across three categories - multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, and pattern disambiguation - with 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, we find that all tested models consistently produced no precautionary warning signal across the tested scenarios where multiple sensors are simultaneously elevated below their individual safety limits, while achieving near-perfect accuracy on single-sensor threshold violations. All five models (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B) score near zero on Category A multi-sensor scenarios (Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592) compared to strong performance on single-sensor scenarios (Category B Q1: 0.975-1.000). Structured tabular formatting shows no consistent advantage over plain prose; ChatGPT-4o performs significantly better under prose (p = 0.001). These findings have direct implications for practitioners deploying the tested models in physical safety monitoring systems.

</details>


### [130] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WSD，一个基于Wasserstein距离的半监督文本属性图数据蒸馏框架，通过图-文本协同编码、图草图化和LLM文本合成模块，在保持性能的同时大幅压缩图规模，解决了现有方法在可扩展性、标签稀缺和可读性方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAG表示学习方法在与大语言模型结合时存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈；已有的数据蒸馏方法无法捕捉图与文本模态间的复杂交互，在半监督场景下标签稀缺，且无法生成可供下游LLM任务读取的人类可读文本属性。

Method: 提出WSD框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 图-文本协同编码模块，采用双路径编码器（图感知和图无关）在协同自训练方案中获取可靠伪标签并融合互补特征；(2) 基于Wasserstein距离的图草图化算法；(3) 基于聚类的关键词提取的低成本LLM文本合成模块，为压缩节点生成连贯、可读的摘要。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，WSD在GNN和LLM下游任务中均取得了最优的性能-压缩权衡，实现了有效且高效的TAG学习与分析。

Conclusion: WSD框架通过理论驱动的Wasserstein距离指导的图草图化和LLM文本合成，有效解决了TAG数据蒸馏中的多模态交互、标签稀缺和可读性问题，为大规模TAG学习提供了一种数据中心的解决方案。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了影响大语言模型欺骗性输出检测探针性能的多种因素，包括表征深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示和训练数据的谎言类型学。实验表明，最优表征深度高度依赖数据集，更具表达力的探针相比线性基线只有选择性提升，稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐状态表现相似。关键在于，训练数据和谎言类型的选择会显著改变可检测性，说明欺骗检测是一个高度依赖表征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测大语言模型的欺骗性输出仍是开放问题，现有工作在跨场景（尤其是域外）泛化方面表现不佳——训练于一种谎言类型的探针难以迁移到其他类型的欺骗场景。

Method: 通过补充包含捏造、遗漏和夸大等多样欺骗类型的标注数据集，系统分析了表征深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示和谎言类型学共四个因素对七种探针类型检测性能的影响。

Result: 最优表征深度高度依赖于具体数据集；更具表达力的探针相比线性基线仅有选择性提升；稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐状态表现相近；训练数据和谎言类型的选择会显著改变可检测性。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测是一个高度依赖表征的问题，选择适当的训练数据和谎言类型学对于构建有效的检测探针至关重要。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [132] [Enabling Scalable Topology Inference in Distribution Systems via Constrained Multi-Source Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20480)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Muhao Guo,Jiaqi Wu,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束推理框架，利用异构证据（电气测量、空间约束、物理运行约束）对配电网拓扑进行修正，而非从零重建。通过检测不一致分配、局部重新连接和迭代物理可行性检查，在三个含8000+AMI电表的馈线上实现95%以上的拓扑重建精度，显著降低计算开销，并引入伪造驱动的可靠性指标辅助验证优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑识别方法主要依赖电气相似性或空间记录，在高密度馈线和不一致元数据条件下可靠性低。维护准确配电网拓扑对于故障定位、电压分析等关键运行任务至关重要，但实际中异构不完美的公用事业数据使得维持可靠连接记录极具挑战。

Method: 将拓扑识别建模为约束推理问题，利用异构证据优化基础拓扑。(1) 检测不一致分配；(2) 在约束邻域内执行局部重新连接以确保可扩展性；(3) 迭代强制执行物理可行性以产生运行一致性拓扑估计。同时提出伪造驱动的可靠性指标，评估每个推断连接相对于替代可行分配的支撑强度。

Result: 在美国大型公用事业公司三个馈线（8000+AMI电表）上验证，拓扑重建精度超过95%，计算开销显著低于全局推理方法。发现纯相关性方法在密集城市馈线中产生歧义分配，而结合电气测量与空间、运行约束可实现鲁棒且可扩展的拓扑恢复。

Conclusion: 所提约束推理框架有效结合异构证据，在真实部署条件下实现高精度、可扩展的配电网拓扑识别，可靠性指标可指导验证优先级，对提升电网运维效率具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate distribution system topology is essential for outage localization, voltage analytics, and operation of distribution grids, yet maintaining reliable connectivity records remains challenging in practice due to heterogeneous and imperfect utility data. Existing topology identification methods often rely primarily on electrical similarity or spatial records alone, which become unreliable in dense feeders and under inconsistent metadata conditions. This paper formulates distribution topology identification as a constrained inference problem that refines a utility-provided base topology using heterogeneous evidence while enforcing spatial feasibility and physical operational constraints. Instead of reconstructing connectivity from scratch, the proposed framework detects inconsistent assignments, performs localized reconnection within constrained neighborhoods to ensure scalability, and iteratively enforces physical feasibility to produce operationally consistent topology estimates. In addition, a falsification-driven reliability metric evaluates how strongly each inferred connection is supported relative to alternative feasible assignments, enabling utilities to prioritize verification efforts while preserving system-wide observability. The framework is validated using operational data from three feeders comprising more than $8{,}000$ AMI meters in collaboration with a large U.S. utility. Results demonstrate over $95\%$ topology reconstruction accuracy while significantly reducing computational effort compared with global inference approaches. The study further shows that correlation-based methods alone produce ambiguous assignments in dense urban feeders, whereas combining electrical measurements with spatial and operational constraints enables robust and scalable topology recovery under realistic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [133] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARGO是一种无需训练的路由框架，通过利用本地模型在推理时对采样响应的一致性信号，决定何时本地执行、何时卸载到云端，在多种任务和模型上超越其他免训练基线，甚至部分超越有监督训练的路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地-云端协作部署大语言模型的方法通常依赖训练好的路由器或协作感知的微调，这些方法将路由行为绑定到特定的运行模式，缺乏灵活性和可迁移性。本文旨在探索是否可以通过本地模型自身的推理时行为（而非额外训练）来实现有效的路由决策。

Method: 提出CARGO框架：1）通过提示变体采样估计本地模型在推理时对生成响应的一致性；2）应用贝叶斯早期停止机制实现样本高效的置信度控制；3）通过轻量级部署时校准，支持任意目标协作比率。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在多种推理和问答任务、多个本地LLM系列和规模、预训练和微调模型上，CARGO一致优于其他免训练基线，并且在若干设置下超越有监督学习训练的路由器。

Conclusion: 有效且适应性强的本地-云端协作可以直接从本地模型的内在响应行为中涌现，无需额外的训练路由器，这表明本地模型自身的响应一致性是一个可靠且通用的路由信号。

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [134] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PersonaTrail基准测试和PACMem框架，用于解决网络智能体在用户指令不明确时，从原始浏览历史中推断用户偏好和回忆信息的问题。PACMem将原始浏览历史分解为事实记忆和偏好记忆，实验表明其优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法捕捉用户指令不明确时，智能体需要从用户原始浏览历史中推断缺失上下文的个性化需求，因为现有基准要么局限于完全明确的提示，要么将历史简化为抽象形式。

Method: 提出Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem)框架，将原始浏览历史分解为两类结构化记忆：事实记忆（总结单个会话）和偏好记忆（提炼重复行为模式）。推理时检索最相关的记忆条目以指导个性化导航。

Result: 在PersonaTrail基准测试上，PACMem在两个任务上均持续优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。

Conclusion: PACMem框架能有效利用原始浏览历史实现网络智能体的个性化，PersonaTrail可作为评估个性化网络智能体的标准基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [135] [Tractable Hierarchical Control of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20483)
*Max Scribner,Antonio Vergari,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多项式时间算法，用于约束自回归大语言模型生成符合LR(k)上下文无关文法的输出，相较于以往指数级时间方法有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将语言模型集成到正式系统（如程序合成）中时，需要确保生成代码和数据的语法有效性。现有方法应用于LR(k)文法时计算复杂度为指数级，效率低下。

Method: 将LLM蒸馏为可处理的概率模型，通过掩码和引导机制约束自回归生成过程，使其满足LR(k)文法的逻辑约束。论文证明了有限持续时间的LR(k)文法的满足性可在多项式时间内计算。

Result: 证明了任何有限持续时间的LR(k)文法的满足性可在多项式时间内计算，优于之前方法的指数级时间。

Conclusion: 该结果使得能够高效地约束和引导LLM生成更符合形式语法约束的输出，为LLM在程序合成等需要语法严格性的任务中的应用提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Constraining the generation of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) is an important component of integrating language models into formal systems. In the generation of code and data for tasks like program synthesis, ensuring that language models produce syntactically valid output is a prerequisite for processing such output. These languages (such as SQL or JSON) are often designed as $LR(k)$ context-free grammars. By distilling the LLM to a tractable probabilistic model, its autoregressive generation can be steered and masked to incorporate the probability of satisfying logical constraints, ensuring high quality output that is guaranteed to be valid. This paper demonstrates that the satisfaction of any $LR(k)$ grammar of finite duration can be calculated in polynomial time, an improvement over the exponential time of applying previous methods to such grammars. This result enables efficient constraint and steering of LLM generation towards output that better satisfies formal syntactic constraints.

</details>
