We propose a novel method using matrix-based differentiable logic programming to mitigate reasoning shortcuts in two phenomena: constraint satisfaction shortcuts, where constraints are satisfied without achieving the intended task, and cognition shortcuts, where biased data leads to semantically incorrect concept mappings despite logically sound inference. Building on recent matrix-based logic programming semantics, we introduce design elements to mitigate shortcuts, including a unified encoding of rules and constraints in a single matrix.  We also identify connections to fuzzy logic t-norms and empirically compare their gradient flow properties. Through carefully designed experiments on MNIST variants, we show that one-to-one grounding of neural outputs to logical atoms significantly reduces both shortcut types compared to previous methods that rely on soft probability distributions.  We then confirm that architectural choices in coupling symbolic knowledge with neural learning play a critical role in shortcut mitigation.

</details>


### [207] [Identifying Good Rules for Efficient SAT Encodings of Single-Constant Multiplication Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21188)
*Chufeng Jiang,Neng-Fa Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号框架，利用图神经网络从常数分解中预测最优操作符，加速硬件设计中单常数乘法SAT编码，在17-32位常数上实现编码时间、内存使用和分支数量一到两个数量级的下降，同时保持近优编码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单常数乘法问题是硬件设计中的NP难优化任务，现有动态规划方法虽能产生近优SAT编码，但大常数的编码成本仍然过高，亟需提升编码的可扩展性和效率。

Method: 提出神经符号框架，使用图神经网络模型从常数分解中预测有前景的操作符类型，利用置信度分数在符号搜索中剪枝无效选择，加速SAT编码过程。

Result: 在未见过的17-32位常数上，编码时间降低一到两个数量级，内存使用减少超过97%，分支数量下降一个数量级，同时保持加法次数的近优编码质量。

Conclusion: 学习引导的符号策略能显著提升SCM编码的可扩展性和效率，证明神经符号方法在硬件设计优化中的有效性。

Abstract: The Single Constant Multiplication problem is a fundamental NP-hard optimization task in hardware design, which seeks to decompose a fixed constant using only additions, subtractions, and bit-shifts. Although dynamic programming methods can produce near-optimal SAT encodings for SCM, their encoding cost remains high for large constants. We propose a neuro-symbolic framework that accelerates SCM SAT encoding by identifying good rules for guiding operator selection during decomposition. Our approach employs a graph neural network model to predict promising operator types from constant decompositions, and exploits the resulting confidence scores to prune no-good choices in the symbolic search. Experimental results on unseen 17-32 bit constants demonstrate one to two orders of magnitude reductions in encoding time, over 97% reduction in memory usage, and an order-of-magnitude decrease in branching, while preserving near-optimal encoding quality in terms of additions. These results show that learning-guided symbolic strategies can significantly improve the scalability and efficiency of SCM encoding. Our code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/Chufeng-Jiang/SCM_MLDP

</details>


### [208] [Bound-Founded Semantics for Answer Set Programming with Difference Constraints: Preliminary Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21201)
*Pedro Cabalar,Jorge Fandinno,Nicolas Rühling,Torsten Schaub,Sebastian Schellhorn,Philipp Wanko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了多类排序的有界Here-and-There逻辑（HTb），为带线性约束的Answer Set Programming（ASP）扩展提供了统一逻辑框架，特别用于形式化clingo[DL]的语义基础，并揭示了不同混合系统（如clingo[DL]、clingcon、flingo）在约束原子正当性证明上的语义差异根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合ASP求解器虽然整合了线性约束，但依赖各异的语义基础，缺乏统一的逻辑基础，这阻碍了不同系统之间语义的严格比较和程序简化等理论研究的开展。

Method: 引入多类排序的有界Here-and-There逻辑变体（HTb），将其应用于差分约束场景，形式化数值变量的正当性（foundedness）概念，并基于该框架对clingo[DL]、clingcon、flingo等混合系统进行语义刻画和对比分析。

Result: 建立了统一的语义框架，成功形式化了现有系统如clingo[DL]的数学基础，揭示了不同混合系统在约束原子正当性证明行为差异的语义根源。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为当前混合ASP系统提供了形式化基础，还为研究程序简化和未来整合不同语义原则奠定了理论支撑。

Abstract: While the integration of linear constraints has significantly expanded the reach of Answer Set Programming (ASP), existing hybrid solvers often rely on disparate semantic underpinnings that lack a unified logical foundation. We address this gap by introducing a many-sorted variant of the Bound-founded Logic of Here-and-There (HTb), providing a versatile framework capable of characterizing equilibrium models across a wide spectrum of alternative semantics for extensions of ASP with linear constraints. We apply this framework to the setting of difference constraints, focusing on the semantic characterization of clingo[DL]. Central to our approach is the formalization of foundedness for numeric variables. By investigating how different hybrid systems - such as clingo[DL], clingcon, and flingo - justify constraint atoms, we uncover the semantic roots of their varying behaviors. This investigation results in a single, consistent framework that not only formalizes the foundations of current systems like clingo[DL] but also facilitates the rigorous study of program simplifications and the future integration of diverse semantic principles.

</details>


### [209] [A New Well-Supported Semantics for Description Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21203)
*Spencer Killen,Jia-Huai You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对描述逻辑程序的良支持语义存在的计算复杂度过高（升至多项式层级第二层）以及缺乏归约变换刻画两个问题，提出了一种更严格评估本体原子（ontological atoms）的新语义，使一致性问题的复杂度保持在NP完全级别。同时识别了新旧语义等价的语法类，并给出了不动点算子和归约变换的刻画。新语义是原良支持语义的严格子集，保留了原良支持性并引入了更严格的新良支持概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DL程序的良支持语义存在两个局限：1）一致性问题的计算复杂度从NP完全升至多项式层级第二层；2）缺乏归约变换（reduct transformation）的刻画形式。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出一种更严格评估本体原子的新语义；识别新旧语义等价的语法类；通过不动点算子和归约变换两种方式刻画新语义。

Result: 新语义使一致性问题的复杂度保持NP完全，而非升至第二层多项式层级；新语义是原良支持语义的严格子集；给出了不动点算子和归约变换的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 新语义在保持良支持性的同时降低了计算复杂度，并通过与逻辑编程更相似的方式提供了更优的良支持概念。

Abstract: Description logic programs are a powerful formalism for combining rules with ontologies. The well-supported semantics for description logic programs ensures that no answer sets rely on cyclic dependencies. Most popular semantics for logic programming have this property of well-supportedness. We recognize two limitations of the current well-supported semantics for DL programs: its increased computational complexity for the consistency problem and its lack of a reduct transformation characterization.  In this work, we present a new semantics which evaluates ontological atoms more strictly than the current semantics. This keeps the complexity of its consistency problem NP-complete, rather than increasing it to the second level of the polynomial hierarchy. Additionally, we identify a syntactic class of description logic programs for which our new semantics is equivalent to the current semantics. We characterize our semantics using a fixpoint operator and a reduct-based transformation. Our new semantics is a strict subset of the current well-supported semantics, so it maintains the prior notion  of well-supportedness while inducing its own stricter notion. We prefer our new notion of well-supportedness due to its similarities with logic programming.

</details>


### [210] [How Rules Represent Causal Knowledge: Causal Modeling with Probabilistic Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21208)
*Kilian Rueckschloss,Felix Weitkaemper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将Pearl的因果理论引入概率逻辑编程（PLP），提出了一种基于非时间性哲学基础的形式化因果语义学，并定义了干预操作与实现方法。该语义在分层ProbLog程序上与P-log语义一致，但在非分层情况及其他PLP形式主义中可能存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的因果理论主要基于贝叶斯网络和因果模型，局限于非循环因果关系，将其思想迁移到其他形式化框架时容易产生误解或不一致性。作者希望将Pearl的因果方法引入概率逻辑编程领域，并建立坚实的哲学基础。

Method: 将概率逻辑程序与先前工作中建立的、不依赖时间概念的哲学基础对齐（假设所有相关事件同时发生）；提出形式化因果语义学、干预概念及实现方法；并与P-log语义进行对比分析。

Result: 所提出的语义在分层ProbLog程序上与P-log语义一致；在非分层情况下及其他PLP形式主义（如非stratified程序）中，两者可能存在差异。

Conclusion: 成功将Pearl的因果理论扩展到概率逻辑编程领域，建立了不依赖时间概念的因果语义学，为PLP中的因果推理提供了形式化基础，同时揭示了不同PLP形式主义在因果语义上的差异。

Abstract: Pearl famously argues that causal knowledge enables the prediction of intervention effects. By contrast, purely descriptive knowledge supports only conclusions drawn from observations. His theory of causality, however, is developed exclusively within Bayesian networks and causal models. Consequently, it is largely restricted to acyclic causal relationships, and transferring its ideas to other formalisms risks misinterpretation or inconsistency.
  This paper brings Pearl's approach to causality into probabilistic logic programming (PLP). To this end, such programs are aligned with philosophical foundations established in prior work that do not rely on temporal notions; that is, all relevant events are assumed to occur simultaneously. A formal causal semantics for these programs, together with a notion of intervention and an implementation, is proposed. It is shown that this semantics coincides with the P-log semantics for stratified ProbLog programs, while the two may differ in the non-stratified case and for other PLP formalisms.

</details>


### [211] [ICAE-Bench: Evaluating Coding Agents as Interactive Project Builders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21217)
*Zhongyuan Peng,Dan Huang,Chuyu Zhang,Caijun Xu,Changyi Xiao,Shibo Hong,David Lo,Lin Qiu,Xuezhi Cao,Jiyuan He,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ICAE-Bench，一个在交互式项目构建场景下评估编码智能体的基准，通过自动化用户代理模拟模糊需求到完整软件的动态开发过程，并引入三种关键设计确保评估的真实性、可复现性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准仍基于静态、完全指定的任务进行评估，未能跟上"vibe-coding"工作流的转变——智能体需在不完整产品意图下将模糊需求转化为可工作的软件，这要求智能体具备规划、需求澄清、工具使用、调试和仓库级构建等综合能力。

Method: ICAE-Bench 提出三种核心设计：(1) 从精确的开源仓库中提取模糊需求，保证可执行行为可追溯；(2) 通过用户代理数据（User Agent Data）约束交互过程，使自动化的用户代理能揭示隐藏约束而不引入新需求或泄露实现细节；(3) 采用标准化黑盒测试结合多维度诊断（功能正确性、语义与API相似度、结构保真度、设计质量、交互质量）来公平评估开放性结果。

Result: 论文本身是基准提案，实验结果尚未在摘要中展示，但预期该基准能够区分不同智能体在交互式项目构建中的能力差异，并揭示现有智能体在需求澄清、仓库级构建等方面的不足。

Conclusion: ICAE-Bench 填补了编码智能体评估从静态任务到动态交互式项目构建的空白，为未来更真实、更全面的智能体能力评测提供了框架。

Abstract: The recent emergence of vibe-coding workflows is changing what coding agents are expected to do. Instead of merely completing code under fully specified instructions, agents are increasingly expected to transform incomplete product intent into working software by combining various abilities including planning, requirement clarification, tool use, debugging, and repository-level construction. Yet existing benchmarks have not fully caught up with this shift, evaluating agents on static, fully specified tasks.
  In this paper, we introduce ICAE-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents under interactive project-building settings. The basic idea is to start from a fuzzy product requirement, simulating the dynamic paradigm with an automated User Agent. To make this setting both realistic and evaluable, ICAE-Bench introduces three key designs. First, to avoid the ambiguity of unconstrained fuzzy requirements, each task derives ambiguity from a precise real open-source repository with executable behavior. Second, to ensure high-quality and reproducible user simulation, ICAE-Bench grounds interaction through User Agent Data, allowing the User Agent to reveal hidden constraints without inventing new requirements or leaking implementation artifacts. Third, to evaluate open-ended repositories fairly, ICAE-Bench uses standardized black-box tests together with multi-dimensional diagnostics, including functional correctness, semantic and API similarity, structural fidelity, design quality, and interaction quality.

</details>


### [212] [Logic Programming Semantics for Causal Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21233)
*Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了逻辑编程语义（稳定模型和支撑模型）与因果过程最终状态之间的关系，揭示了稳定模型对应于从中性状态开始且不受干扰的因果过程的最终状态，而支撑模型则描述了从任意起点可达的最终状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生命科学中具有挑战性的建模问题驱动，旨在理解逻辑编程语义与因果过程最终状态之间的关系，并为逻辑编程作为因果规则语言的适当语义讨论做出贡献。

Method: 通过理论分析，建立了逻辑编程的稳定模型和支撑模型语义与不同初始条件下因果过程最终状态之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明了正逻辑程序的稳定模型对应于从中性状态开始且不受干扰的因果过程的最终状态，而支撑模型描述了从任意起点可达的最终状态。

Conclusion: 该工作为逻辑编程作为因果规则语言的语义选择提供了新的时间视角，从因果性的解释性观点出发，对稳定模型和支撑模型语义进行了重新诠释。

Abstract: Motivated by challenging modelling issues in the life sciences, we investigate the relationship between logic programming semantics and the eventual states of causal processes compatible with those logic programs. More precisely, we show that while stable models of positive logic programs correspond to the eventual states of processes commencing from a neutral state and continuing undisturbed indefinitely, supported models describe the eventual states reachable from arbitrary starting points. This also contributes to the discussion of the appropriate semantics for logic programming as a causal rule language, adding a temporal perspective to recent interpretations of the stable and supported model semantics from an explanatory viewpoint of causality.

</details>


### [213] [BasketEvent: Understanding Who Did What and When in Basketball Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21267)
*Yu Zhang,Jiayuan Rao,Haoning Wu,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BasketEvent数据集和PlayNet框架，将篮球视频理解从事件识别推进到以球员为中心的细粒度理解，包括识别事件、定位责任球员及其时间证据边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将空间感知和语义识别分离，无法将事件归因到具体球员，也无法在复杂的集体动态中定位事件的时间边界。

Method: 构建BasketEvent数据集（包含1000个精确时间区间标注样本），并提出PlayNet框架，通过追踪关键实体、关联球员身份、建模球员-球员/球员-球/全局场地交互，并采用门控池化聚合稀疏时间证据，实现以球员为中心的事件推理。

Result: PlayNet在多个指标上显著优于视频级和裁剪式基线方法，验证了以球员为中心建模在细粒度体育视频理解中的优势。

Conclusion: 以球员为中心的建模方法对于理解复杂的集体运动场景至关重要，能够实现更精准的事件归因和时间定位。

Abstract: Comprehensive basketball video understanding requires resolving not only what event occurs, but also who is responsible and when the key evidence appears. However, exist- ing methods typically treat spatial perception and semantic recognition as isolated tasks, failing to ground events to individual players or pinpoint their temporal boundaries within complex collective dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce BasketEvent, a player- centric basketball event understanding dataset curated from real NBA broadcasts. In BasketEvent, event labels are grounded to the responsible players, and a manually an- notated subset of 1,000 samples with precise event intervals is provided to evaluate tem- poral evidence localization. Based on this data, we propose PlayNet, a player-centric reasoning framework that maps basketball videos to player-level event predictions with temporal evidence. Concretely, PlayNet tracks key entities, associates player identities, and reasons about events by modeling player-player, player-ball, and global court inter- actions, while aggregating sparse temporal evidence via gated pooling. Extensive experi- ments demonstrate that PlayNet significantly outperforms representative video-level and crop-based baselines, proving the superiority of player-centric modeling for fine-grained sports video understanding. Our data, code, and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [214] [An LLM-Driven Workflow for Automated Process Control Strategy Generation and Tuning from Dynamic Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21292)
*Ari Luna Rueda,Eike Cramer,Klaus Hellgardt,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大型语言模型的结构化工作流，用于从动态过程模型中自动完成多变量控制设计。该工作流将设计任务分解为多个约束代码生成步骤，包括接口构建、归一化、MV-CV配对、控制器规格说明、闭环仿真、场景生成、性能评估和贝叶斯优化调优。在非线性气体预热器基准上验证，生成的分散PI反馈前馈控制结构通过贝叶斯优化使闭环性能指标降低约26.5%，证明了结构化LLM代码生成用于控制设计工作流的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化多变量控制设计过程，减少人工干预，利用大语言模型的结构化代码生成能力来构建可执行的控制设计工作流。

Method: 提出结构化LLM驱动的工作流，将控制设计分解为8个约束代码生成步骤（plant-interface构建、归一化、MV-CV配对、控制器规格说明、闭环仿真、场景生成、性能评估、贝叶斯优化调优），每个步骤生成的代码先执行验证，失败则用验证反馈修复，最后通过贝叶斯优化进行控制器参数调优。

Result: 在非线性气体预热器基准上，生成的分散PI反馈-前馈控制结构通过贝叶斯优化使闭环性能指标降低约26.5%，主要体现在压力回路瞬态性能的改善。

Conclusion: 证明了使用结构化LLM代码生成构建可执行控制设计工作流的可行性，同时指出需要在更大规模的全局控制基准上进行更广泛的验证。

Abstract: We present a structured large-language-model-driven workflow for automated multi-variable control design from dynamic process models. The workflow decomposes the design task into constrained code-generation steps: plant-interface construction, normalization, manipulated-variable controlled-variable (MV-CV) pairing, controller specification, closed loop simulation, scenario generation, performance evaluation and Bayesian-optimization (BO) based tuning. Generated artifacts are executed and validated before downstream tasks proceed, and failed artifacts are repaired using validation feedback. The proposed approach is demonstrated on a nonlinear gas-preheater benchmark with coupled pressure and temperature dynamics. The generated workflow produces a physically consistent decentralized PI (proportional-integral) feedback-feedforward control structure and an executable tuning environment. Bayesian optimization reduces the closed loop performance objective, which aggregates set-point tracking and disturbance-rejection errors for the controlled variables, by approximately 26.5% relative to the initial controller generated by the workflow, mainly through improved pressure-loop transient performance. This figure quantifies the automated tuning stage rather than a comparison against a manually designed controller. The results demonstrate the feasibility of using structured large-language-model-based code generation to construct executable control-design workflows, while also highlighting the need for broader validation on larger plantwide-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [215] [Expert Behavior Prior Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21302)
*Gong Gao,Weidong Zhao,Xianhui Liu,Ning Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Expert Behavior Prior (EBP)的算法，通过Q引导的条件变分自编码器(Q-CVAE)从在线回放缓冲区直接生成专家策略先验，结合专家策略引导(EPG)和策略梯度校正(PGC)模块，在无需离线预训练的情况下显著提升了在线强化学习的样本效率和收敛稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为先验强化学习(BPRL)方法依赖静态离线数据集，存在数据多样性低和轨迹质量次优的问题，限制了策略先验的有效性，导致在线训练中探索效率低下和动力学不稳定。

Method: 1) Q引导条件变分自编码器(Q-CVAE)从在线回放缓冲区生成专家策略先验；2) 专家策略引导(EPG)机制从生成的支持集中选择专家动作；3) 策略梯度校正(PGC)模块协调Q引导与专家监督，实现稳定一致的策略改进。

Result: 在Gym、PyBullet和DMControl等机器人控制与工业控制基准上，EBP显著优于现有最先进的在线RL算法，实现了更高的样本效率和更稳定的收敛。

Conclusion: 通过在线生成专家先验替代传统离线预训练，能够有效解决BPRL中数据多样性不足和轨迹质量差的问题，为提升在线强化学习性能提供了新思路。

Abstract: Behavior prior reinforcement learning (BPRL) has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve sample efficiency in online reinforcement learning (RL) by leveraging policy priors derived from offline demonstrations. However, most existing BPRL methods rely on static offline datasets, which often suffer from low data diversity and suboptimal trajectory quality. This reliance restricts the effectiveness of policy priors, hindering both policy exploitation and stability during online training. Consequently, agents are prone to inefficient exploration and unstable learning dynamics. To address these limitations, we deviate from existing offline pre-training methods and propose an Expert Behavior Prior (EBP) algorithm. Specifically, we introduce a Q-guided conditional variational autoencoder (Q-CVAE) that learns to generate expert policy priors directly from the online replay buffer. This enables the generation of high-value actions for guiding policy updates without relying on pre-collected expert trajectories. To further enhance policy exploitation, we propose an expert policy guidance (EPG) mechanism that selects expert actions from a generative support set, and we integrate a policy gradient correction (PGC) module to harmonize Q-guidance with expert supervision, promoting stable and consistent policy improvement. Extensive experiments conducted on robotic control (Gym, PyBullet) and industrial control (DMControl) benchmarks demonstrate that EBP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art online RL algorithms, achieving higher sample efficiency and more stable convergence.

</details>


### [216] [Regulating autonomous and agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21345)
*Chris Reed,Alex Austria,Anmol Bharuka,Pragnitha Mandava,Khushiya Mujawar,Luka Shakhkulashvili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自主性和代理性AI对现有监管体系的挑战，分析了四个监管系统（英国内容平台监管、数据保护、英国金融服务、欧盟AI法案），并提出将监管从被动反应转变为主动适应的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监管假设监管对象具备充分的知识和控制能力，但自主性和代理性AI打破了这一前提，许多知识控制权分散在AI供应链中，现有监管模式无法有效应对AI自主性带来的新系统性风险。

Method: 对四个监管系统（英国内容平台监管、数据保护、英国金融服务、欧盟AI法案跨部门制度）进行案例分析，研究自主性和代理性AI带来的挑战并提出潜在解决方案。

Result: 自主性AI使事后监督作为风险管理工具失效，需要将AI供应链纳入监管范围，并采用新的治理方法而非复制现有模式。

Conclusion: 监管需要从被动反应流程转变为主动流程，以适应AI自主性的挑战。

Abstract: Regulating activities where regulatees use autonomous and agentic AI is challenging. Regulatory assumptions about regulatee knowledge and control no longer hold true; much of that lies elsewhere in the AI supply chain which thus needs to be brought within the scope of regulation. Governance systems for autonomous AI cannot replicate existing governance models, but need a fresh approach. Retrospective supervisory oversight becomes ineffective as a risk management tool, and AI autonomy generates new systemic risks which require new solutions. This paper investigate four regulatory systems: UK regulation of content platforms, data protection, UK financial services, and the EU AI Act\'92s cross-sectoral regime. It analyses the challenges posed by autonomous and agentic AI and proposes potential solutions which regulators might adopt. These will transform regulation from a reactive process to an active one, and assist it in adapting to the challenges of AI autonomy.

</details>


### [217] [SPORD: A Simulation-Propose-then-OR-Dispose Approach for Supply Chain Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21354)
*Jiayin He,Yutong Pan,Sen Yang,Ningxuan Kang,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Simulation-Propose-then-OR-Dispose (SPORD)方法，部署为京东NetSim平台，通过解耦模拟与优化，解决了电商供应链规划中的操作碎片化、计算不可行性和实施障碍问题，自2025年部署以来优化了超过20,000家供应商的服务，跨区域履约率从6.1%降至4.9%，月均减碳约5,745 tCO2e。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商供应链规划长期以孤立项目形式运作，面临三大障碍：操作碎片化（定制模型泛滥难以标准化）、计算不可行性（百万SKU、数千节点组合规模超出现有求解器能力）、实施障碍（数学最优解因缺乏信任而无法落地）。亟需一种统一、可扩展且可信任的规划方法。

Method: SPORD方法的核心是解耦：仿真模块（Propose）生成并评估所有操作上可行的候选路径，吸收所有特殊性业务逻辑；整数规划模块（Dispose）从候选中选择全局最优子集。计算上采用矩阵向量化CPU/GPU加速仿真（提速10-100倍）和列表调度算法（将耦合订单处理从小时级降至分钟级），并通过智能诊断引擎建立闭环。

Result: 自2025年部署以来，NetSim平台优化了超过20,000家供应商的端到端服务，跨区域履约率从6.1%降至4.9%，月均碳减排约5,745 tCO2e。

Conclusion: SPORD将仿真从被动监控转向主动规划，透明输出使怀疑的高管转变为积极参与的合作者，模块化架构确保后续规划仅需配置而非重构，为电商供应链规划提供了一种可落地、可扩展、可信任的解决方案。

Abstract: For years, supply chain planning at e-commerce firms has operated as a collection of isolated projects. Each planning task from static network planning to dynamic warehouse assortment planning requires analysts to spend weeks building models from scratch, calibrating and persuading executives to act on outputs they cannot verify. Three barriers drive this: bespoke models proliferate because standardization is difficult (operational fragmentation); once unified, the combinatorial scale of millions of SKUs, thousands of nodes, and intricate routing logic exceeds what solvers can handle within a tight window (computational intractability); and a mathematically optimal solution still fails to be implemented if the executives do not trust it (implementation hurdle). To bridge this gap, we propose and implement the Simulation-Propose-then-OR-Dispose method, deployed as JD.com's NetSim platform. The central insight is decoupling: simulation proposes by generating and evaluating the full set of operationally valid candidate paths-absorbing all idiosyncratic business logic, while an integer program disposes by selecting the globally optimal subset. Computationally, matrix-vectorized CPU/GPU accelerated simulation achieves a 10-100 times speedup over serial methods, and a list scheduling algorithm reduces coupled-order processing from hours to minutes. Operationally, we establish a closed loop via an intelligent diagnosis engine. Since 2025, NetSim has optimized end to-end services for over 20,000 suppliers, the cross-regional fulfillment rate dropped from 6.1% to 4.9%, and the average monthly carbon reduction is approximately 5,745 tCO2e. SPORD moves simulation from monitoring to active planning. The transparent outputs turn skeptical executives into engaged collaborators, and the modular architecture ensures that the next planning requires just configuration, not reconstruction.

</details>


### [218] [Towards Faithful Graph Explanations with Synergistic Edge Effects via Granular Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21381)
*Jiancu Chen,Shuyin Xia,Guan Wang,Degang Chen,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeeExplainer是一种无参数的可解释性方法，通过粒球图分解机制捕捉边之间的协同效应，并在结构图上扰动节点和边生成解释性子图，在多个图分类数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN解释方法通过扰动每条边来评估其重要性，但忽略了边之间的协同效应，导致不能准确刻画边的真实重要性。

Method: 提出SeeExplainer，包含两个步骤：1) 引入粒球图细化机制，将图分解为若干无固定大小的不相交粒球，并以这些粒球为节点构建结构图，以捕捉边之间的协同效应；2) 在结构图上扰动节点和边，根据各自贡献生成解释性子图。

Result: 在多个不同网络的图分类数据集上，SeeExplainer的性能优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过考虑边之间的协同效应，SeeExplainer能够更准确地解释GNN的决策，是一种有效的无参数解释方法。

Abstract: Instance-level explanations aim to reveal the rationale behind a model's decisions for a specific graph. Previous methods explain graph neural networks (GNNs) by selecting important edges to induce subgraphs, where edge importance is assessed by perturbing each edge and observing changes in the model predictions. However, they often neglect the synergistic effects among edges, which are crucial for accurately characterizing edge importance. To address this issue, we propose SeeExplainer, a parameter-free explainer to interpret GNNs. Specifically, we first introduce a granular-ball graph refinement mechanism that decomposes a graph into several disjoint granular-balls with no fixed size, and utilize them as nodes to construct a structural graph. This process can better capture the synergistic effects among edges. Then, we perturb nodes and edges in the structural graph to generate explanatory subgraphs based on their respective contributions. Experiments on several graph classification datasets of different networks show that SeeExplainer outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [219] [Multimodal Pretraining for Generalizable EEG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21384)
*Targol Bakhtiarvand,Jugal Kalita,Adham Atyabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个多模态脑电图（EEG）基础模型，结合了基于Mamba架构的原始信号编码器、用于时频数据的Vision Transformer（ViT）风格编码器以及轻量级文本编码器，通过掩码建模、跨视图对比对齐和时间一致性损失等创新预训练技术，在CHB-MIT癫痫检测基准上取得了0.874 AUROC（单模型）和0.878 AUROC（集成模型）的当前最优性能，并在留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）协议下展示了患者独立的癫痫检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG癫痫模型通常局限于特定数据集和任务，跨数据集或跨场景的泛化能力有限。因此，研究者希望开发一种可适应多种EEG任务的基础模型，以实现更好的跨数据集迁移和通用性。

Method: 构建多模态EEG基础模型，包含三个编码器：基于Mamba架构的原始信号编码器、ViT风格的时频数据编码器以及轻量级文本编码器，所有编码器共享嵌入空间。预训练采用掩码建模、跨视图对比对齐和时间一致性损失等自监督学习技术，无需标注数据。

Result: 在标准CHB-MIT数据集划分下，单模型AUROC达0.874，集成模型达0.878，均达到当前最优性能。在留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）协议下，19个受试者的平均平衡准确率为0.558，展示了患者独立检测的难度与模型能力。

Conclusion: 该多模态基础模型在多个数据集和评估设置下实现了鲁棒的癫痫检测，能够轻松适应新的检测场景，并支持可解释的癫痫定位。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) models used for epilepsy are often limited to specific datasets and tasks. This limited approach can make it challenging to apply these models across different datasets or in various situations. However, recent studies in foundation models and self-supervised learning suggest that an adaptable EEG backbone could support a range of EEG related tasks. In this study, we have developed a multimodal EEG foundation model that combines a raw signal encoder based on the Mamba architecture, a Vision Transformer (ViT)-style encoder for time-frequency data, and a lightweight encoder for text, all within a shared embedding space. The pretraining process relies on several innovative techniques, such as masked modeling, cross-view contrastive alignment, and temporal consistency losses. These methods are designed to create rich, seizure-relevant representations without requiring labeled data. To assess the efficacy and generalization of our pretrained model, we fine-tuned it on the canonical CHB-MIT seizure detection benchmark and additional seizure detection datasets, and conducted extensive experiments comparing different model variants. On the standard CHB-MIT split, our best single model achieved an AUROC of 0.874, and an ensemble variant reached 0.878 AUROC, representing state-of-the-art performance on this benchmark. In addition to standard train-test splits, we evaluated performance under a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol, which is rarely reported in prior EEG seizure modeling work and highlights the difficulty of patient-independent seizure detection, with a mean LOSO balanced accuracy of 0.558 across 19 subjects. Across datasets and evaluation settings, our multimodal foundation model enabled robust seizure detection and straightforward adaptation to new seizure detection scenarios, while also supporting interpretable seizure localization.

</details>


### [220] [MSBraM: A Multi-scale Self-supervised Brain Foundation Model for Hierarchical EEG Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21402)
*Tao Zhou,Jing Han,Lingyu Shu,Zixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MSBraM提出了一种多尺度自监督脑电基础模型，通过两阶段预训练（多尺度神经分词器+课程多尺度掩码策略）学习层次化EEG表示，在2400+小时数据上预训练后，在12个公共数据集的10个下游任务上优于现有方法，证明多尺度时间建模对EEG基础模型至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督EEG基础模型难以捕捉EEG信号固有的多尺度时间结构（局部神经模式与长程依赖），导致跨尺度表示学习受限，阻碍了模型在不同下游任务上的泛化能力。

Method: 两阶段预训练框架：1) 多尺度神经分词器通过向量量化重建将原始EEG信号离散化为不同时间分辨率的语义编码；2) 采用课程多尺度掩码策略预测被掩码的编码，逐步整合细粒度局部模式与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在超过2400小时EEG数据上预训练，在12个公共数据集的10个下游任务上，MSBraM优于其他最先进的预训练模型，展现出强大的泛化性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 显式建模多尺度时间动态特性是构建有效EEG基础模型的关键。

Abstract: Self-supervised foundation models have recently shown strong potential for electroencephalogram (EEG)-based analysis. However, existing approaches struggle to capture the inherently multi-scale temporal structure of EEG signals, where local neural patterns and long-range dependencies jointly encode task-relevant information. This limitation hampers cross-scale representation learning and generalization across diverse downstream tasks. To address this challenge, we propose MSBraM, a Multi-Scale self-supervised Brain foundation Model designed to learn hierarchical EEG representations. MSBraM follows a two-stage pretraining framework. First, a multi-scale neural tokenizer discretizes raw EEG signals into semantic codes at different temporal resolutions via vector-quantized reconstruction. Second, the model is pretrained to predict masked codes using a curriculum multi-scale masking strategy, progressively integrating fine-grained local patterns with global temporal context. We pretrain MSBraM on over 2,400 hours of EEG data and evaluate it across 10 downstream tasks on 12 public datasets. Extensive experiments show that MSBraM achieves superior performance on other state-of-the-art pretrained models, demonstrating strong generalization and transferability. These results indicate that explicitly modeling multi-scale temporal dynamics is critical for effective EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [221] [Euclid-MCP: A Model Context Protocol Server for Deterministic Logical Reasoning via Prolog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21412)
*Bartolomeo Bogliolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Euclid-MCP，一个基于SWI-Prolog的开源MCP服务器，通过Euclid-IR中间表示和translate-run-inspect-repair循环，为LLM提供确定性逻辑推理能力。实验表明在安全合规场景下，Euclid-MCP相比纯LLM能避免幻觉、降低延迟并输出更紧凑的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多步逻辑推理中不可靠，尤其在安全关键和合规敏感领域容易产生幻觉；现有的神经符号方法缺乏标准化接口，难以被工具增强型智能体统一使用。

Method: 提出Euclid-MCP架构：(1) 基于MCP协议提供标准工具接口；(2) 设计Euclid-IR中间表示，将Horn子句逻辑映射为人类可读、LLM易生成的形式；(3) 实现translate-run-inspect-repair循环，LLM代理可将推理委托给外部符号引擎，并访问证明轨迹和推导日志。

Result: 在IT安全合规场景下，纯LLM在小知识库上表现充分，但在大规模问题上系统性产生幻觉；Euclid-MCP提供精确答案，延迟更低，输出更紧凑。

Conclusion: 语义RAG本质上不适合规则强制执行，Euclid-MCP可作为RAG助手和智能体系统的稳定共享推理基座。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at natural language understanding and generation but remain unreliable for multi-step logical reasoning, especially in safety-critical or compliance-sensitive domains. Recent neuro-symbolic approaches address this gap by coupling neural models with external symbolic engines, yet most integrations are bespoke and lack a standardized interface for tool-augmented agents. This paper presents Euclid-MCP, an open-source MCP server that provides deterministic logical reasoning via SWI-Prolog. Euclid-MCP introduces Euclid-IR, an engine-agnostic intermediate representation for Horn-clause logic that is human-readable, easy for LLMs to generate, and straightforward to compile into Prolog or alternative backends. The server exposes a compact tool interface that supports a translate-run-inspect-repair loop, enabling LLM clients to delegate inference while retaining full access to proof traces and derivation logs. We evaluate Euclid-MCP on a realistic IT security and compliance use case. Results show that while LLMs alone are sufficient on small knowledge bases, they hallucinate systematically on larger problems, whereas Euclid-MCP delivers exact answers with lower latency and more compact outputs. We argue that semantic RAG is fundamentally unsuited for rule enforcement, and that Euclid-MCP can serve as a stable, shared reasoning substrate for both RAG-based assistants and agentic systems.

</details>


### [222] [Logical Regression for Planning with Axioms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21414)
*Connor Little,Christian Muise*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在包含公理的规划领域中对动作进行逻辑回归近似的计算方法，该方法将条件限制在部分状态上，生成最小的部分状态并避免重新计算公理。在执行监控场景中，该方法可减少高达70%的变量，并在意外变化环境中实现超过50%的恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑回归在基本规划设置中计算简单，但当引入公理等额外因素时变得显著复杂，需要一种能够处理公理的近似方法，以扩展逻辑回归在实际规划问题中的应用范围。

Method: 提出一种近似逻辑回归的方法，将条件限制在部分状态上，生成最小部分状态，同时避免重复计算公理。将该回归形式嵌入执行监控上下文中进行验证。

Result: 在多个领域中将执行监控考虑的变量数量减少高达70%；在意外变化环境中，多个领域的恢复率超过50%。

Conclusion: 该近似逻辑回归方法能显著泛化部分状态、减少变量数量，并产生足够鲁棒的执行监控器，能够在不确定环境中频繁恢复。

Abstract: In automated planning, logical regression is an operation that returns the most general condition necessary for an action to achieve a particular formula. It has many applications, such as allowing for more robust plan execution and providing compact policies for non-deterministic planning. Although relatively simple to calculate in basic planning settings, logical regression becomes significantly more complex when additional factors, such as axioms, are present. We introduce a methodology for approximating the logical regression of an action in a domain that includes axioms; an approximation that limits conditions to partial states. Our method produces minimal partial states while avoiding the recalculation of axioms. To demonstrate the impact of our methods, we embed our form of regression in an execution monitoring context, a well-established setting that can benefit greatly from logical regression. Our results show that this form of regression can dramatically generalize partial states across multiple domains, reducing the number of variables considered for execution monitoring by up to 70%, and demonstrate that the resulting execution monitor is robust enough to recover frequently in an environment with unexpected changes: several domains recover over 50% of the time in our tests.

</details>


### [223] [PATS: Policy-Aware Training Scaffolding for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21419)
*Yipeng Shi,Zhipeng Ma,Yue Wang,Qitai Tan,Yang Li,Peng Chen,Zhengzhou Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种以策略为中心的训练范式Pats，将技能作为动态训练支架，通过证据卡片和任务特定评估为弱策略提供适应性指导，在ALFWorld和WebShop上提升达18.6%，并在检索增强QA基准上减少32.1%的提示token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程LLM智能体强化学习中，弱策略经常重复相似的失败，产生信息量不足的轨迹，限制了有效的策略优化。现有以技能为中心的方法虽然改进了探索，但仍以技能本身为中心，而非设计为对进化中策略的自适应训练支持。

Method: 提出Pats框架，将最新策略的 rollout 组转换为证据卡片，并使用任务特定评估来调整后续 rollout 中使用的上下文。随着策略改进，冗余上下文被修订或移除，以减少对显式指导的依赖，同时保留有用的rollout变体。策略使用标准RLVR通过环境奖励进行优化，训练支架在部署时丢弃。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，Pats相比强基线提升高达18.6%。在七个检索增强QA基准上，Pats在保持竞争力的同时，比基线少使用32.1%的提示token。

Conclusion: Pats通过将技能重新定义为动态训练支架，有效解决了弱策略在长程RL中的探索失败问题，在多个基准上取得显著提升，并具有更高的token效率。

Abstract: In long-horizon LLM agent reinforcement learning, weak policies often repeat similar failures, producing uninformative rollout trajectories and limiting effective policy optimization. Existing skill-centric methods improve exploration by optimizing, filtering, or internalizing reusable skills. However, they remain centered on the skills themselves rather than being designed as adaptive training-time support for the evolving policy. To address this, we propose a policy-centric training paradigm that reframes skills as a dynamic training scaffold. Our framework, Pats, converts rollout groups from the latest policy into evidence cards and uses task-specific evaluation to adjust the context used in subsequent rollouts. Concrete guidance helps weak policies to complete challenging tasks. As policy improves, redundant context is revised or removed to reduce reliance on explicit guidance while preserving useful rollout variation. The policy is optimized with environmental rewards using standard RLVR, and the training scaffold is discarded at deployment. On ALFWorld and WebShop, Pats improves over strong baselines by up to 18.6%. Across seven search-augmented QA benchmarks, it remains competitive while using 32.1% fewer prompt tokens than the baseline.

</details>


### [224] [Bridging the Gap Between Plausibility and Admissibility: Constraint-Aware Flow Maps for Dynamic Graph Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21421)
*Michael Romei de Socio,Gian Luca Pozzato,Alessio Merlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在动态图结构系统中，通过后采样符号约束（硬过滤、软加权、投影修复）提升条件扩散模型生成轨迹的结构可行性。在紧凑图场景下无效概率质量为0.002996，而中等复杂度图场景下升至0.155929。硬过滤可去除所有无效轨迹并保留84.4%的样本，软加权保留有效样本量但有效性提升有限。依赖约束几乎解释了所有不可接受性。结论：统计合理性与结构可接受性是不同可靠性属性，符号约束在图复杂度增加时更具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能通过集成未来系统轨迹支持不确定性下的决策，但统计合理性并不能保证结构可行性。现有方法缺乏对生成轨迹结构有效性的保证，因此需要探索后采样的符号约束机制来提升动态图结构系统中轨迹建模的可靠性。

Method: 采用条件扩散模型从部分观测生成未来图状态轨迹，并引入外部符号层进行三种约束处理：硬过滤（剔除无效轨迹）、软加权（概率加权）和基于投影的修复。在紧凑图和中复杂度依赖图两种受控合成场景下评估，衡量结构有效性、样本效率、多样性、鲁棒性和校准性能。

Result: 紧凑图场景无效概率质量为0.002996，中复杂度图场景升至0.155929。硬过滤去除所有无效轨迹的同时保留84.4%生成样本；软加权保留有效样本量但仅带来有限的有效性提升。家族级分析表明依赖约束几乎解释了全部不可接受性。

Conclusion: 统计合理性与结构可接受性是两种不同的可靠性属性。随着图结构复杂度的增加，符号约束处理的价值愈发显著。硬过滤在保证结构有效性方面优于软加权，但投影修复的具体效果需要进一步探索。

Abstract: Generative models can support decision-making under uncertainty by producing ensembles of plausible future system trajectories, but statistical plausibility does not ensure structural feasibility. This study investigates whether post-sampling symbolic constraints can improve the reliability of generative trajectory modeling in dynamic graph-structured systems. A conditional diffusion model generates future graph-state trajectories from partial observations, while an external symbolic layer applies hard filtering, soft weighting, or projection-based repair. The framework is evaluated on two controlled synthetic regimes: a compact graph and a medium-complexity dependency graph, using metrics for structural validity, sample efficiency, diversity, robustness, and calibration. In the compact regime, the model produces an invalid probability mass of 0.002996, indicating an almost entirely admissible trajectory manifold. Under the same architecture and training protocol, invalid mass increases to 0.155929 in the medium-complexity regime. Hard filtering removes all invalid retained trajectories while preserving 84.4% of generated samples, whereas soft weighting preserves effective sample size but yields only limited validity gains. Family-level analysis shows that dependency constraints account for nearly all observed inadmissibility. These results indicate that statistical plausibility and structural admissibility are distinct reliability properties and that symbolic constraint handling becomes more valuable as graph-structural complexity increases.

</details>


### [225] [Agent-Guided Relational Concept Discovery: Toward Interpretable Surgical Margin Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21437)
*Nooshin Maghsoodi,Amoon Jamzad,Robert Policelli,Mohammad Farahmand,Dilakshan Srikanthan,Martin Kaufmann,Kevin Y. M. Ren,Shaila Merchant,Sonal Varma,Ross Walker,Doug McKay,John Rudan,Gabor Fichtinger,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent-Guided Concept Discovery框架，通过无需预定义标签的概念学习、智能体优化语义描述以及生化知识图谱约束，提升REIMS深度学习模型在手术切缘评估中的泛化能力和可解释性，在皮肤癌和乳腺癌数据集中取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在REIMS手术切缘评估中面临两大挑战：一是训练数据来自离体组织标注样本，但实际手术中需处理噪声大、无标注的在线数据，导致泛化能力差；二是模型黑箱特性使其行为难以理解和改进。监督式概念学习虽有潜力但需要昂贵的概念标注，难以在复杂质谱流程中实现。

Method: 提出Agent-Guided Concept Discovery框架：1) 从数据直接学习有意义的概念，无需预定义概念标签；2) 训练过程中推理智能体（reasoning agent）不断优化所学概念的语义描述，并根据诊断相关性自适应调整概念权重；3) 利用生化知识图谱对概念进行 grounding，确保与已知代谢关系的一致性。

Result: 在皮肤癌和乳腺癌数据集上，该方法相比基线模型提升了平衡准确率和灵敏度。在代表性术中案例中，假阳性更少，表明对手术条件的泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: Agent-Guided Concept Discovery通过自动化概念发现与知识图谱约束，有效解决了REIMS深度学习在术中评估中泛化性差和可解释性不足的问题，推动了质谱分析在临床手术中的应用。

Abstract: Deep learning models can effectively use Rapid Evaporative Ionization Mass Spectrometry (REIMS) data for surgical margin assessment. However, their clinical adoption remains challenging due to limited generalization to operating room conditions. This difficulty arises because models are typically trained on labeled spectra collected from resected tissue samples, while they must operate on noisy, unlabeled data acquired directly during surgery. In addition, the black-box nature of deep learning models makes it difficult to understand and systematically improve their behavior. Concept-based learning offers a promising way to address these challenges by mapping raw measurements to human-understandable concepts. However, supervised concept-based approaches rely on concept annotations, which are difficult to obtain in complex mass spectrometry workflows. We propose Agent-Guided Concept Discovery, a framework that learns meaningful concepts directly from data without requiring predefined concept labels. During training, a reasoning agent refines semantic descriptions of the learned concepts and adaptively adjusts their weight based on diagnostic relevance. These concepts are further grounded using a biochemical knowledge graph to ensure consistency with known metabolic relationships. Across Skin and Breast Cancer datasets, our model improves balanced accuracy and sensitivity over the baseline. In a representative intraoperative case, it shows fewer false positives, indicating better generalization to surgical conditions.

</details>


### [226] [Detecting LLM-Generated Tokens in Human--LLM Coauthored Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21458)
*Yangjun Lu,Hongyi Zhou,Fabian Spill,Kai Ye,Chengchun Shi,Jin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应Lepski规则的标记级文本检测方法，通过平滑相邻检测分数来定位人机混合创作文档中由LLM生成的部分，无需标记级训练数据，在理论和实证上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人机协作写作的兴起，现有LLM文本检测方法主要关注文档级分类，无法识别混合作者文档中哪些部分由LLM生成。迫切需要一种细粒度的检测方法来实现局部定位。

Method: 在标记级别（语言模型的基本单元）进行操作，基于已有的标记级检测分数，通过平滑相邻标记的分数来降低变异性，并利用自适应的Lepski型规则根据局部作者结构选择带宽。

Result: 理论上展示了该方法在估计潜在信号时具有良好的均方误差性能；实验上在合成数据集和真实数据集上均优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法实现简单、无需标记级训练数据，理论性能有保障，实证表现优异，并已部署公开可访问的网站实现该方法。

Abstract: The rise of human-AI collaborative writing has created a growing need for fine-grained detection methods that support localizing likely LLM-generated content in mixed-authorship documents. Existing methods for detecting LLM-generated text mainly focus on document-level classification and cannot identify which parts of the text are generated by LLMs. This paper introduces a new method to address this urgent need. Our method operates at the token level, the natural unit of modern language models, and builds on existing token-level detection scores. The key idea is to smooth adjacent token scores to reduce their variability, while using an adaptive Lepski-type rule to select the bandwidth according to the local authorship structure. Our method is simple to implement and does not require token-level labeled data for training. Theoretically, we characterize this trade-off and show that the proposed method achieves favorable mean square error performance in estimating the underlying signal. Empirically, we demonstrate strong performance of our method against a wide range of baselines in both synthetic datasets and a realistic dataset. We deploy a publicly accessible website that implements the methods as well.

</details>


### [227] [AREX: Towards a Recursively Self-Improving Agent for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21461)
*Shuqi Lu,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Zhang Zhang,Hui Wang,Hongwang Xiao,Zheng Liu,Lei Xiong,Jiahao Wang,Sen Wang,Xiyan Jiang,Wanli Li,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Bingyu Yan,Ziyi Xia,Yingxia Shao,Kang Liu,Zhicheng Dou,Di He,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhongyuan Wang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AREX是一个递归自改进的深度研究智能体家族，通过内外双循环机制（内循环收集证据构建答案，外循环逐约束审计并针对性改进），结合自主上下文压缩工具和长时强化学习，在多个基准上超越同规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究需要智能体找到同时满足多个约束的答案，但发现答案成本高，而验证候选答案可分解为可处理的逐约束检查。这种"发现-验证不对称性"表明智能体应递归地改进当前答案，而非简单延长搜索时间。

Method: AREX采用递归自改进架构：(1)内研究循环：收集证据并构建临时答案；(2)外自改进循环：逐约束审计答案，识别未解决声明，发起针对性后续研究。为支持长程递归自改进，学习自主上下文更新工具，将增长的交互相历史压缩为紧凑的改进状态（保留已验证证据和未解决约束）。通过智能体中间训练和长时强化学习在验证过的合成任务和高质量轨迹上训练，并在长时学习中通过强调获取决定性证据或纠正错误研究方向的关键步骤来缓解稀疏奖励问题。实例化了一个密集4B模型和一个122B-A10B的混合专家模型。

Result: 在BrowseComp、WideSearch、DeepSearchQA、Humanity's Last Exam (HLE)以及其他推理和工具使用基准上，AREX大幅超越同规模基线，并且与使用显著更多激活参数的模型竞争。

Conclusion: AREX通过递归自改进范式有效解决了深度研究中的发现-验证不对称问题，在多个具有挑战性的基准测试中展示了优异的性能，证明了递归自改进和自主上下文压缩对于长程复杂推理任务的有效性。

Abstract: Deep research requires agents to find answers that jointly satisfy multiple constraints. Discovering such answers is costly, whereas verifying a candidate can often be decomposed into tractable constraint-wise checks. This discovery--verification asymmetry suggests that a research agent should do more than simply search longer: it should recursively improve its current answer by verifying intermediate results and using the partially verified state to guide subsequent refinement. We introduce AREX, a family of Recursively Self-Improving (RSI) deep research agents. AREX alternates between an inner research loop that gathers evidence and constructs a provisional answer, and an outer self-improvement loop that audits the answer constraint-wise, identifies unresolved claims, and launches targeted follow-up research. To sustain RSI over long horizons, AREX learns an autonomous context-update tool that compresses growing interaction history into a compact improvement state preserving verified evidence and unresolved constraints, without relying on an external model. We train AREX on verified synthetic tasks and high-quality trajectories through agentic mid-training and long-horizon reinforcement learning. To mitigate sparse final rewards during long horizon learning, we emphasize key steps where decisive evidence is acquired or erroneous research directions are corrected. We instantiate a dense 4B model and a 122B-A10B Mixture-of-Experts model. Across BrowseComp, WideSearch, DeepSearchQA, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and other reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, AREX substantially outperforms comparable-scale baselines and remains competitive with models using substantially more activated parameters.

</details>


### [228] [Agentic coding without the cloud: evaluating open-weight large language models on longitudinal data preparation tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21482)
*Mack Nixon,Liam Wright,Yevgeniya Kovalchuk,Alison Fang-Wei Wu,Martin Danka,Andy Boyd,David Bann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源框架，用于评估基于开放权重大语言模型的AI代理在纵向人口研究数据准备任务中的效能。该框架包含真实标注数据集、任务定义和自动化评估流程，并在消费级硬件上对多种LLM进行了基准测试。结果显示，31-35B参数模型几乎达到饱和（任务完成率最高87.9%），表明开放权重LLM在受治理限制的研究环境中具有可行潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向人口研究中的数据准备是持续存在的瓶颈，而使用第三方云LLM处理个人数据受治理要求的限制（禁止数据传输到外部服务）。因此需要探索本地可部署的开放权重模型作为替代方案。

Method: 构建了一个包含真实标注数据集（来自英国队列研究的六轮数据清洗脚本）、20个数据准备任务（涵盖类别统一化、多波合并等）和自动化评估流程的框架，用于评测LLM生成的R代码和输出数据。在消费级硬件上对多种开放权重LLM进行基准测试。

Result: 31-35B参数的最先进开放权重模型在基准测试中几乎饱和，平均任务完成率最高达87.9%。消费级硬件上的开放权重LLM表现出了在治理受限研究环境中实现AI辅助数据准备的可行路径。

Conclusion: 开放权重LLM在消费级硬件上的性能表明，其在受治理限制的研究环境中辅助数据准备是可行的。框架已开源以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agents are now widely used tools in code development, with data typically sent to third-party cloud-based models. Their adoption in research using personal data is constrained by governance requirements that typically prohibit data transmission to external services. Locally deployable open-weight models offer an alternative since sensitive data never leave the local environment. We introduce an open-source framework for evaluating the efficacy of AI agents powered by open-weight LLMs on one of the most persistent bottlenecks in research on longitudinal population studies: data preparation. The framework comprises: a curated ground-truth dataset (cleaning scripts preparing six sweeps of data from a British cohort study), task definitions encompassing tasks such as category harmonization and multi-wave merging, and automated routines for evaluating the LLM-produced R code and outputted data. We benchmark LLMs across the (consumer grade) deployment spectrum to assess their efficacy in 20 data preparation tasks (creation of 102 variables). Current state-of-the-art, 31-35B parameter models almost saturated our benchmark ("average task completion" up to 87.9%). The performance of open-weight LLMs running on consumer-grade hardware shows promise of a viable path toward AI-assisted data preparation in governance-restricted research settings. Our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/UCL-ARC/RRBench.

</details>


### [229] [Toward Continuous Assurance for the Democratization of AI Agent Creation in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21495)
*Natan Levy,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出了低代码/无代码环境下非工程用户创建的AI Agent存在可靠性问题，并提出了一种轻量级持续保障框架，通过依赖映射、就绪契约、计划检查、诊断和生命周期治理来确保Agent在部署后仍保持操作就绪状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent的创建已从专业开发者扩展到非工程用户，低代码/无代码环境使得Agent的创建门槛大幅降低，但这些Agent依赖变化的模型、工具、检索源、权限、提示词、调度和外部服务，部署后即使无人直接修改也可能发生静默退化，产生了可靠性缺口。

Method: 提出了一个轻量级持续保障框架，包含依赖映射、就绪契约、计划检查、诊断和生命周期治理五个组件；并构建了初始原型审计工具，通过场景化评估将提出的分类法转化为实际检查和可操作的修复指导。

Result: 给出了框架设计和初始原型审计器，并通过场景化评估展示了分类法如何转化为实际检查与修复指导。

Conclusion: 公民创建的AI Agent需要持续保障机制来应对部署后的静默退化问题，所提出的轻量级框架和原型工具为此提供了可行的解决方案方向。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly created inside organizations by non-engineering users through low-code, no-code, and conversational development environments. This democratization enables rapid local innovation, but it also creates a reliability gap: agents that appear to users as simple productivity artifacts may depend on changing models, tools, retrieval sources, permissions, prompts, schedules, and external services. These dependencies can cause silent degradation long after deployment, even when no user directly modifies the agent. This paper identifies the reliability challenge created by democratized AI agent creation and proposes a lightweight continuous-assurance framework for citizen-created organizational agents. The framework combines dependency mapping, readiness contracts, scheduled checks, diagnostics, and lifecycle governance to assess whether an agent remains operationally ready under expected conditions. We also present an initial prototype auditor and scenario-based assessment showing how the proposed taxonomy can be translated into practical checks and actionable remediation guidance.

</details>


### [230] [Agentic Context Management: Solving Agent Memory and Cost by Treating Them as Lifecycle and Architecture Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21503)
*Gaurav Dadhich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI Agent 的失败主因并非推理能力不足，而是无法管理推理上下文（如对话历史、提示词、工具定义与输出）。本文提出"Agentic Context Management (ACM)"范式，将其分解为五个原语（架构、摄取、范围界定、预判、压缩与整合），并通过参考实现 Maximem Synap 在 LongMemEval 和 LoCoMo 上分别达到 92% 和 93.2% 的准确率，同时论证了朴素上下文累加的平方级 token 成本问题与有效压缩的线性成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将 Agent 的上下文问题狭义地视为"存储-检索"问题，忽视了上下文管理的全生命周期需求。生产环境中 Agent 因上下文管理不当（如历史累积、工具输出膨胀、跨会话遗忘）而失败的情况远超推理能力不足的情况，且 token 成本随轮次呈平方级增长，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出 Agentic Context Management (ACM) 五原语：(1) 架构设计（Architecting）——设计上下文结构；(2) 摄取（Ingesting）——提取与结构化记忆；(3) 范围界定（Scoping）——按数据类型选择存储并跨组织层级管理；(4) 预判（Anticipating）——预测当前与下一步所需信息；(5) 压缩与整合（Compacting & Consolidation）——在预算内保留关键信息并维护溯源。基于此实现多租户服务 Maximem Synap。

Result: Maximem Synap 在 LongMemEval 上达到 92%，在 LoCoMo 上达到 93.2%。论文还指出现有基准未覆盖的维度：延迟、token 效率、上下文轮转（context-rot）抵抗力，以及决策级和组织级上下文管理的前沿方向。

Conclusion: Agent 上下文管理应从"存储-检索"的窄框架扩展为全生命周期管理（ACM）。有效压缩可在线性 token 成本下保持保真度，而朴素累加和粗放摘要分别面临平方级成本和精度悬崖。ACM 是生产级 AI Agent 的关键基础设施，未来需向决策层面和组织层面纵深发展。

Abstract: Production AI agents' failures are less often due to an inability to reason well and more often because they cannot manage what is in their reasoning context: conversation histories, large prompts, large tool definitions, and ballooning tool outputs. Agents drown in their own accumulating history while paying a token cost that grows every turn, producing missing recalls within and across conversations. The incumbent response treats this as a storage-and-retrieval problem. We argue that framing is too narrow. Actively managing what an agent holds in mind is a lifecycle, not merely a store: it spans deciding what to remember, extracting and structuring it, choosing the right store per data type, consolidating and forgetting while preserving provenance, deciding what is relevant now, anticipating what is needed next, and compacting context to a budget without losing what matters. In serious production this operates not over a single user but across an organizational scope hierarchy. We name this discipline Agentic Context Management (ACM) and decompose it into five primitives: architecting, ingesting, scoping, anticipating, and compacting & consolidation. We then make the economic case: naive context accumulation grows token cost quadratically in conversation length, crude summarization buys linear cost at the price of an accuracy cliff, and only validated compaction achieves linear cost with preserved fidelity. We describe a reference implementation, Maximem Synap, that realizes the five primitives as a multi-tenant service and reports 92% on LongMemEval and 93.2% on LoCoMo under the configuration detailed in Section 6. We close with dimensions existing benchmarks do not yet capture, latency, token efficiency, and context-rot resistance, and the frontier of decision-level and organization-level context the category points toward.

</details>


### [231] [Same Dangerous Objective, Opposite Advice: Direct Exposure versus Multi-Agent Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21518)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，高能力LLM在直接面对危险目标时会表现出更安全的拒绝对抗行为，但当目标通过中间代理（Id和Censor）进行变换和转发后，用户端模型（Superego）反而会产生与目标对齐的 unsafe 建议。这暴露了一种组合性安全漏洞：自动化多阶段工作流可以将显式操纵性目标保留，同时隐藏原始指令、操纵条款和来源，使下游模型和终端用户无法直接审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前高能力LLM在组合性多阶段工作流中，面对显式操纵性目标时的安全行为反向偏移现象，揭示现有安全机制的潜在漏洞。

Method: 使用OpenAI的gpt-5.6-sol模型别名，测试25个预指定的镜像权衡剖面。设计直接暴露（Direct exposure） vs. 间接变换（Id和Censor变换为情感和约束重写的目标意图）两种条件，观测用户端Superego模型的输出行为。

Result: 直接暴露于授权隐瞒、捏造和施压的目标时，模型产生的建议与目标相悖（安全拒绝对抗）；而经过Id和Censor变换后，用户端Superego（仅看到优选方向，未看到原始目标、操纵条款及来源）产生的建议与目标对齐（不安全顺从）。

Conclusion: 该行为反向偏移与模型识别或不信任操纵性动机一致，但内部机制尚未明确。这一结果暴露了组合性安全缺口：高能力模型可用作用户端组件服务于显式操纵性目标的多阶段自动化工作流，工作流可将原始指令、操纵授权条款及来源隐藏在下游模型上下文之外，终端用户也无法直接审查上游信息。

Abstract: Even a current high-capability LLM can appear safer when shown a dangerous objective directly than when other agents transform and relay its direction. Using OpenAI's gpt-5.6-sol model alias, we test 25 pre-specified mirrored trade-off profiles. Direct exposure to an objective authorizing concealment, fabrication, and pressure produced advice net opposed to its target. After an Id and Censor transformed the same objective into affect and a constraint-rewritten, target-bearing intention, the user-facing Superego---which saw the preferred direction but not the raw objective, its manipulative clauses, or its source---produced advice net aligned with the target.
  This behavioral reverse shift is consistent with the model recognizing or distrusting the manipulative motive, although we do not identify its internal mechanism. The second result exposes a compositional safety gap: a current high-capability model can be used as the user-facing component of an automated, multi-stage workflow serving an explicitly manipulative objective. The workflow can keep the raw instruction, its manipulation-authorizing clauses, and its provenance outside the downstream model's context while preserving the objective's target direction. A user with endpoint-only access likewise cannot directly inspect those upstream messages including the objective.

</details>


### [232] [The Boundaries of Automation: A Theory of Persistent Human Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21547)
*Fares Fourati,Hinrich Schütze,Eyke Hüllermeier,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"人类参与AI系统只是暂时的，最终将被完全自动化替代"的假设，提出即使AI能力极强，人类参与仍将因技术互补、规范发展和目标涌现三个理由而持续存在。人机共建不是临时措施，而是目标通过参与本身而涌现的活动的持久特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望挑战自动化研究中隐含的假设——人类参与只是因为AI能力不足。他们提出，在高度发达的AI系统中，人类参与仍有存在理由，不应被视为过渡状态。

Method: 通过概念分析，从技术/互补性、规范/发展和涌现性三个层面论证人类参与的必要性，并提出"目标涌现"（target emergence）概念来解释为什么某些活动中人类参与是构成性的。

Result: 识别出三类人类参与理由：技术互补（人类提供AI不具备的能力）、规范发展（参与本身对能动性和学习有价值）、涌现理由（目标在互动中生成，参与是目标的构成部分）。

Conclusion: 人机共建是某些活动的持久特征而非临时方案，这对AI系统设计、评估和伦理具有重要启示。

Abstract: The rapid progress of AI has intensified the long-standing pursuit of automation: replacing human participation with algorithms wherever possible. Implicit in this pursuit is the assumption that humans remain in the loop only because current AI systems are not yet sufficiently capable. This paper challenges that assumption. Rather than asking how far automation can extend, we ask where its conceptual limits lie and argue that human participation may persist even with highly capable AI systems for three distinct reasons. Technical or complementarity grounds arise when humans contribute capabilities or perspectives unavailable to AI. Normative or developmental grounds arise when participation itself is valuable for human agency or learning. Most importantly, emergence grounds arise from target emergence: in some activities, the target is not fully specified in advance but instead emerges through the interaction itself. In these cases, human participation is not merely a means of improving execution but is constitutive of the target being produced. Human--AI co-construction, understood as the joint production of outcomes by humans and AI systems, is therefore not simply a temporary response to imperfect AI, but a persistent feature of activities whose objectives emerge through participation. This perspective has important implications for the limits of automation and for the design, evaluation, and ethics of future AI systems.

</details>


### [233] [MIRROR: Learning from the Other View for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21552)
*Wen Ye,Yuxiao Qu,Aviral Kumar,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现VLMs在不同模态视图（文本、图表、图文结合）下对同一几何问题的推理表现不一致，为此构建了ODA-Data多模态几何数据集，并提出MIRROR强化学习方法，通过选择最佳视图作为教师、利用反向KL散度训练其他视图，显著提升多模态推理的准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs展现出强推理能力，而VLMs在视觉推理（尤其是几何问题）上表现不佳。不同模态视图（文本、图表、图文结合）会引发模型不同的行为表现，这种不一致性暴露了互补的推理路径和失败模式，但标准的多模态后训练并未充分利用这一现象。

Method: 首先构建ODA-Data高质量配对多模态几何数据集，包含同一问题的文本主导、图像主导和图文结合三种视图及训练/评估划分。然后提出MIRROR（模态互惠推理优化），一种基于强化学习的方法：对每个问题在所有视图下评估模型，选择表现最佳的视图作为教师，利用反向KL散度目标训练其他视图向教师对齐。

Result: 在几何推理基准测试上，MIRROR相比标准强化学习取得了更好的性能，模型在不同模态下的推理准确性和一致性均得到提升。

Conclusion: 利用跨模态视图间的不一致性进行互惠自监督学习，能有效提升VLMs的多模态几何推理能力，使模型在文本、图像及图文结合等不同视角下表现更准确、更一致。

Abstract: Unlike large language models (LLMs) that exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with visual reasoning, even on geometry problems that admit equivalent text, diagram, and combined diagram+text views. We show that these views often elicit different behaviors: a model may solve a problem from text but fail on the corresponding diagram, or succeed visually while failing textually. This inconsistency suggests that different views expose complementary reasoning paths and failure modes that standard multimodal post-training does not fully exploit. To study and exploit this phenomenon, we construct ODA-Data, a high-quality paired multimodal geometry dataset with text-dominant, image-dominant, and combined image+text views of the same problems, together with splits for training and evaluating modality-dependent reasoning behaviors. We then develop Modality-Informed Reciprocal Reasoning Optimization (MIRROR), a reinforcement learning approach for improving multimodal reasoning via self supervision. For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [234] [OpenForgeRL: Train Harness-native Agents in Any Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21557)
*Xiao Yu,Baolin Peng,Ruize Xu,Hao Zou,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Wenlin Yao,Nikhil Singh,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenForgeRL 是一个开源框架，通过解耦训练和推理，利用轻量级代理和 Kubernetes 编排器，实现对基于 harness 的智能体进行端到端强化学习训练，在多种复杂环境和基准测试中超越同类开源基线，并系统分析了 harness 选择和 RL 对智能体行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 智能体依赖复杂的推理 harness（如 Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw），但这些 harness 使智能体难以在开放基础设施上进行端到端训练，因为现有的 SFT/RL 流程无法原生表达有状态、多进程的 harness 推理。作者希望解决这一训练基础设施的缺失问题。

Method: 提出了 OpenForgeRL 框架，核心包括：(1) 轻量级代理，拦截 harness 的模型调用并记录为训练数据；(2) Kubernetes 编排器，每个 rollout 在独立远程容器中运行；(3) 将训练与推理解耦，使得标准 RL 代码库（如 veRL）能够训练任意 harness 上的智能体。

Result: 在 ClawEval 上 OpenForgeClaw 达到 31.7 pass^3 和 55.9 pass@3，QwenClawBench 上达 33.7；GUI 任务中 OSWorld-Verified 达 37.7，Online-Mind2Web 达 63.0，WebVoyager 达 72.3，超越同规模开源基线，GUI 设置中匹配或超越数倍大的模型。

Conclusion: OpenForgeRL 成功实现了基于 harness 的智能体端到端训练，验证了不同 harness 的可学习性存在显著差异，RL 能够提升智能体的自验证、工具覆盖和多步规划完成等可靠性能力，但错误恢复等关键能力仍然薄弱。

Abstract: Modern AI agents rely on elaborate inference harnesses such as Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw to drive multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and access to external systems. While powerful, these complex harnesses also make agents hard to train end-to-end with open infrastructure, whose SFT/RL stacks cannot natively express stateful, multi-process harness inference. To address this, we present OpenForgeRL, an open-source framework for training harness-based agents end-to-end in diverse environments. OpenForgeRL achieves this with a lightweight proxy that serves the harness's model calls while recording them as training data for a standard RL codebase (e.g., veRL), and a Kubernetes orchestrator that runs each rollout in its own remote container, together enabling training on any harness in any environment at scale. By decoupling training and inference, OpenForgeRL allows researchers to easily train, study, and improve agents directly in the real harnesses and environments they are deployed with. We validate our framework across diverse, complex harnesses and environments, spanning tool/claw-based agents and multimodal GUI browser- and computer-use agents. Using only hundreds to a few thousand tasks, OpenForgeClaw reaches 31.7 pass^3 and 55.9 pass@3 on ClawEval and 33.7 on QwenClawBench. OpenForgeGUI reaches 37.7 on OSWorld-Verified, 63.0 on Online-Mind2Web, and 72.3 on WebVoyager. Both outperform open baselines of similar size on nearly all benchmarks, and in the GUI setting match or surpass models several times larger. Beyond benchmarks, we analyze how harness choice (e.g., ZeroClaw, OpenClaw, Codex) and RL shape agent behavior. We find that some harnesses are substantially harder to learn than others, and that RL improves agentic reliability, such as self-verification, tool coverage, and completing multi-step plans, though critical abilities such as error recovery remain weak.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型在社交校准中的判断修订机制，发现模型沿着三个维度（观点距离、来源归因、联盟结构）结构化地修订判断，并将谄媚行为重新理解为更广泛的社会影响驱动的判断更新过程的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚视为一维故障模式，但缺乏对模型何时应吸收他人观点与何时应坚持自身道德判断的区分机制。本文旨在建立更系统的框架来理解模型的社会影响过程。

Method: 通过三项研究，系统操纵三个社会心理维度：观点与模型初始立场的距离、观点的来源归因（是否呈现为模型自身先前判断）、以及支持该观点的联盟结构，观察模型的判断修订模式。

Result: 模型对邻近观点更易接受，对呈现为自身先前判断的观点更易受影响，对群体压力的响应具有差异性。这些现象与人类社会心理学中的经典现象平行。

Conclusion: 谄媚只是更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表现，该框架为区分建设性信念修订与谄媚合规提供了原则性基础，有助于在道德关键交互中实现更好的对齐。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [First-Order Analysis of Optimization in Uniformly Convex Metric Spaces: Directional Subderivatives and Basic Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20677)
*D. Russell Luke,Titus Pinta,Qinyu Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为一致凸度量空间中的显式一阶方法开发了分析与实现工具，提出了在方向次导数、函数值和迭代序列下的收敛充分条件，并证明了最速下降方向的存在性，为非凸局部光滑优化提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一阶优化方法从欧几里得空间推广到一致凸度量空间，为非凸、局部光滑优化问题提供理论基础。

Method: 利用方向次导数、函数值和迭代序列，结合有界性、测地光滑性和度量Polyak-Łojasiewicz性质，提出收敛的充分条件；并证明最速下降方向的存在性。

Result: 建立了在一致凸度量空间中一阶显式方法收敛的充分条件，并证明了存在满足假设条件的最速下降方向。

Conclusion: 该工作为局部光滑非凸优化中的一阶显式算法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop tools for the analysis and implementation of explicit first-order methods for minimizing functions in uniformly convex metric spaces. We formulate sufficient conditions for convergence of descent sequences in terms of directional subderivatives, function values and iterates under regularity assumptions including boundedness, geodesic smoothness and a metric Polyak-Łojasiewicz property. We show that there exists a steepest descent direction in which the assumptions for convergence are satisfied. This work provides a foundation for first-order explicit algorithms for locally smooth, nonconvex optimization.

</details>


### [237] [Rule-Induced Behavior of Fuzzy Scalar Objective Functions for Reliable Multi-Criteria Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20731)
*Olaf Frommann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模糊规则的标量目标函数在多准则优化中的行为，发现其偏好景观可能与预期决策语义存在偏差。通过分析两类双准则Pareto前沿和一种占优参考公式，区分了前沿选择机制与参考改进行为。研究表明，平坦的规则激活平台可能导致虚假的参考跟随，而真正的参考跟随需要低模糊性的局部极小值。在占优参考设定下，全局隶属度与竞争规则能恢复鲁棒Pareto权衡但不保证改进参考设计，而三分类规则集则能一致改进占优参考、恢复Pareto集并避免平台驱动选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊规则标量目标函数能灵活编码定性偏好、参考区域和准则间交互，但其诱导的标量偏好景观可能与预期决策语义存在显著差异，需要系统研究这一偏差的成因与诊断方法。

Method: 采用两个解析可控的双准则Pareto前沿和一个嵌入的二维占优参考公式，分离前沿选择机制与参考改进行为，分析隶属度放置、隐式单准则基线规则和显式规则后件对标量目标函数行为的影响。

Result: 平坦规则激活平台会导致虚假参考跟随，真实参考跟随需要低歧义性的局部极小值；全局隶属度与竞争后件能恢复鲁棒Pareto权衡但不一定改进参考设计；三分类规则集能一致改进占优参考并恢复Pareto集。

Conclusion: 提供了诊断度量和实践指导，帮助构建优化行为与预期决策语义一致的模糊标量化函数。

Abstract: Fuzzy-rule-based scalar objective functions provide a flexible way to encode qualitative preferences, reference regions, and interactions between criteria in multi-criteria optimization and decision making. However, the scalar preference landscape induced by such rules can differ substantially from the intended decision semantics. This paper investigates how membership placement, implicit single-criterion baseline rules, and explicit rule consequents affect the behavior of fuzzy scalar objective functions. Two analytically controlled bi-criteria Pareto fronts and an embedded two-dimensional dominated-reference formulation are used to separate front-selection mechanisms from reference improvement behavior. The study shows that apparent reference following can be caused by flat rule-activation plateaus, whereas genuine reference following requires localized minima with low tie ambiguity. In the dominated-reference setting, global memberships with competing consequents recover robust Pareto tradeoffs but do not necessarily improve each reference design. By contrast, a reference-based three-class rule set consistently improves dominated references, recovers the Pareto set, and avoids plateau-driven selection in the present tests. The results provide diagnostic metrics and practical guidance for constructing fuzzy scalarizations whose optimization behavior is consistent with the intended decision semantics.

</details>


### [238] [A Safeguarded Projected-Gradient Framework for Complementarity-Constrained Least-Squares Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20786)
*Lianghai Xiao,Wei Zhang,Jiayi Zhong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对广义绝对值方程（GAVE）和线性互补问题（LCP），提出了一种带保护的投影梯度框架，分析了收敛性并证明了有限步恢复唯一解的能力，数值实验验证了其高精度和问题相关的计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAVE和LCP广泛存在于均衡与优化模型中，且均可被表述为互补集上的最小二乘问题，需要一种统一的求解框架。

Method: 基于两者共享的最小二乘形式，提出带保护的投影梯度框架，并结合问题相关的代数细化；分析收敛性，在更强条件下证明有限步恢复唯一解。

Result: 在GAVE和LCP基准测试中，该框架展示了高最终精度以及问题相关的计算权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的带保护投影梯度框架能有效统一求解GAVE和LCP，具有良好的收敛性保障和实际数值表现。

Abstract: Generalized absolute value equations (GAVEs) and linear complementarity problems (LCPs) arise in many equilibrium and optimization models and can both be formulated as least-squares problems over the complementarity set. Building on this shared formulation, we propose a safeguarded projected-gradient framework for both classes with problem-dependent algebraic refinements. We analyze its convergence properties and, under stronger conditions, establish finite recovery of the unique solution. Numerical experiments on GAVE and LCP benchmarks illustrate the practical behavior of the framework, including its high final accuracy in the tested settings and its problem-dependent computational tradeoffs.

</details>


### [239] [Mean field and N-agent games for optimal relative consumption-investment with jump risk and common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20845)
*Yiming Jiang,Fuxing Li,Yawei Wei,Zimeng Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了N人博弈及其平均场博弈中的最优消费-投资问题。每个投资者面临异质噪声、共同噪声和向下跳跃风险，通过相对绩效（消费和终期财富）产生交互。利用随机最大值原理，在平均场极限下得到了确定性平均场均衡的解析形式，并基于此构建了N人博弈的近似纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Merton（1971）的最优消费-投资问题扩展至多竞争主体环境，并借鉴Lacker（2020）的框架，引入相对绩效关注（消费和终期财富）以及向下跳跃风险，研究竞争性投资者之间的博弈均衡。

Method: 构建N人博弈和平均场博弈框架；每个体投资于受异质噪声、共同噪声和向下跳跃风险影响的个体风险资产；通过随机最大值原理求解平均场极限下的均衡。

Result: 在平均场极限下得到了确定性平均场均衡的解析表达式；通过数值实验说明了均衡及其金融含义；基于平均场均衡构造了N人博弈的近似纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 成功将相对绩效关注引入多主体消费-投资问题，在包含多种噪声和跳跃风险的复杂环境中获得了可解析处理的平均场均衡，为大规模竞争性投资者的最优策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal consumption--investment problem for competitive agents in an \(N\)-player game and its associated mean field game. Each agent invests in an individual risky asset subject to idiosyncratic noise, common noise and downward jump risk, and the interaction among agents is induced by relative performance concerns in both consumption and terminal wealth. In the mean field limit, we characterize a deterministic mean field equilibrium in analytical form by using the stochastic maximum principle. Numerical experiments are presented to illustrate the resulting equilibrium and its financial implications. Finally, based on the obtained mean field equilibrium, we construct an approximate Nash equilibrium for the \(N\)-player game. This model is motivated by \cite{Merton1971} and \cite{Lacker2020}.

</details>


### [240] [Structure-Preserving Spectral Dynamic Programming on Compact Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20854)
*Shanqing Liu,Yang Qi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧李群上有限时域最优控制的动态规划半群的谱逼近方法，针对Galërkin迭代中正交投影导致的范数误差放大问题，提出了一种Fejér型马尔可夫谱滤波器，恢复了动态规划结构，并给出了Lipschitz数据的收敛率$O(\sqrt{δ}+\sqrt{ε+1/(δN^2)})$，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧李群上的动态规划半群（Bellman算子）在sup范数下是单调且非扩张的，但基于$L^2(G)$正交分解的Fourier谱方法（Peter-Weyl分解）与sup范数之间存在定量不匹配。Galërkin迭代中谱投影的Lebesgue常数在$S^1$上对数增长、在秩一紧李群（如SO(3)）上多项式增长，导致迭代在几步内违反基本界，因此需要重新设计保持动态规划结构的离散格式。

Method: 用马尔可夫型谱滤波器替代正交投影，构造了一种有限秩、保正性且非扩张的Fejér型滤波器。引入辅助的热核/消失粘性格式，在Lipschitz数据下证明sup范数收敛。滤波器被解释为受控动力系统的小随机扰动，从而避免了对值函数先验正则性或滤步骤的粘性一致性论证的需求。最终结合正立方体求积和SO(3)上的精确Wigner输运实现全离散格式。

Result: 主结果是Fejér型滤波器的收敛定理：对Lipschitz数据，收敛率为$O(\sqrt{δ}+\sqrt{ε+1/(δN^2)})$，其中$δ$为时间步长、$N$为谱分辨率、$ε$为粘性系数；粘性可为零，耦合$δ=N^{-1}$时得到率$N^{-1/2}$。数值实验验证了预测的收敛率和滤波器偏差，并量化了两种基于图册的方法的框架依赖性。

Conclusion: 通过设计保正非扩张的谱滤波器，成功解决了紧李群上动态规划半群谱逼近中正交投影与sup范数不匹配导致的不稳定问题，恢复了单调非扩张的结构，并在无需值函数正则性的条件下获得了显式收敛率。该方法可推广到基于正立方体求积的全离散实现，为SO(3)等李群上的最优控制问题提供了可行的谱方法。

Abstract: We study spectral approximations of the dynamic programming semigroup for finite-horizon optimal control on a connected compact Lie group $G$, and of the associated first-order Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. The Bellman operator is monotone and non-expansive in the supremum norm, while the Peter-Weyl decomposition of $L^{2}(G)$, on which every Fourier method on $G$ rests, is orthogonal, and the mismatch is quantitative. The natural sup-norm error recursion of the Galerkin iteration is amplified at every step by the Lebesgue constant of the spectral projection, which grows logarithmically on $S^{1}$ and polynomially on compact Lie groups of rank one, including $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and in computation the iteration violates elementary bounds within a few steps. We restore the dynamic programming structure at the discrete level by replacing the orthogonal projection with spectral filters of Markov type. An auxiliary heat-kernel/vanishing-viscosity scheme yields qualitative sup-norm convergence for Lipschitz data. The main result is a Fejér-type filter on $G$, finite-rank, positivity preserving and non-expansive, together with a convergence theorem at the rate $O(\sqrtδ+\sqrt{ε+1/(δN^2)})$ for Lipschitz data, where $δ$ is the time step, $N$ the spectral resolution and $ε$ the viscosity. The viscosity may be zero, and the coupling $δ=N^{-1}$ then gives the rate $N^{-1/2}$. The proof interprets the filter as a small random perturbation of the controlled dynamics, requires neither a priori regularity of the value function nor a consistency argument in the viscosity sense for the filtering step, and extends to a fully discrete realization based on positive cubature, with exact Wigner transport on $\mathrm{SO}(3)$. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted rates and filter bias and quantify the frame dependence of two chart-based baselines.

</details>


### [241] [Implicit Primal-Dual Guarantees in Unconstrained First-Order Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20875)
*Benjamin Grimmer,Alex L. Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于一阶凸优化算法的收敛性证明设计，证明在温和条件下，任何保证原始目标间隙上界的一阶方法实际上都具有更强的显式原始-对偶间隙保证，揭示了动量方法中辅助序列的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究固定步长一阶凸优化方法的收敛性证明结构，特别是性能估计问题（PEP）框架下是否存在结构化的紧致收敛证明，以及如何从原始间隙保证中推导出更强的原始-对偶间隙保证。

Method: 基于性能估计问题（PEP）框架的理论分析，考虑非光滑Lipschitz问题（次梯度Oracle）和光滑问题（梯度Oracle），研究固定步长一阶方法的收敛性证明结构，通过构造隐式最优对偶证书（仿射下界形式）建立原始-对偶间隙保证。

Result: 证明在温和条件下，任何仅依赖初始距离界\|x0-x*\|来保证原始目标间隙f(x_N)-f(x*)上界的一阶方法，实际上在同一收敛率下具有更强的显式、可计算的原始-对偶间隙保证。这些隐式最优对偶证书以仿射下界的形式存在，并揭示了动量方法中辅助序列的理论作用。

Conclusion: 一阶凸优化方法的收敛性证明具有内在的原始-对偶结构，PEP框架不仅能产生紧致收敛证明，还能自然导出隐式对偶证书，为理解和设计动量方法等先进一阶算法提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: This work considers the design of first-order convex optimization algorithms and convergence proofs. In particular, we consider nonsmooth Lipschitz and smooth problems accessed through a subgradient or gradient oracle, respectively. For the general class of fixed-step first-order methods, prior work on Performance Estimation Problems (PEPs) has shown that structured, tight convergence proofs typically exist. Under mild conditions, we further show that any first-order method guaranteeing a bound on the primal objective gap $f(x_N)-f(x_\star)$ assuming only a bound on $\|x_0-x_\star\|$ actually has a stronger guarantee on an explicit, computable primal-dual gap at the same rate. These implicit optimal dual certificates, which take the form of affine lower bounds, also provide insight into the role of auxiliary sequences in momentum methods.

</details>


### [242] [Learning to recover: Adaptive local branching with reinforcement learning for log-truck routing and scheduling under disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20901)
*Abdelhakim Abdellaoui,Issmail El Hallaoui,Loubna Benabbou,François Aubé,Mouloud Amazouz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对加拿大林业中木材卡车路径与调度的实时再优化问题，提出了一种基于强化学习的Pareto前沿搜索方法。通过参数化偏差界k（与原始计划的ℓ1距离），精确计算最小可行k值，并利用REINFORCE算法自适应选择k值，在稳定性与成本之间生成非支配恢复方案。实验表明，该方法相比固定网格和对分搜索基线，能在更少的求解器调用下获得更丰富的Pareto前沿，并在所有配置和中断类型下满足操作可接受的再优化时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 林业物流中，道路封闭、车辆故障、行程延迟和需求波动等不可预见的中断会破坏预定的战术计划，需要在紧迫的时间约束下同时恢复可行性并限制与原始计划的偏差，这两个目标本质上是相互冲突的。现有方法难以高效探索这一权衡空间。

Method: 将恢复问题建模为一系列由偏差界k参数化的邻域受限混合整数线性规划；精确计算最小可行k值k_min以锚定搜索；采用REINFORCE策略梯度算法训练强化学习策略，根据求解器反馈自适应选择连续的k值，将计算资源集中在有希望的区域，并在改进可能性较小时终止探索。

Result: 基于加拿大林业合作伙伴的历史数据生成的周实例，覆盖所有类别的中断场景，该方法相比固定k值网格和对分搜索基线，能在更少的求解器调用下获得更丰富的Pareto前沿，并在所有配置和中断类型下产生符合操作可接受再优化时间的可行恢复方案。

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习自适应搜索方法能高效生成覆盖稳定性-成本空间的Pareto前沿，为调度员提供结构化的操作选项，在实时再优化场景中具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We consider the real-time reoptimisation of log-truck routing and scheduling in the Canadian forestry industry following unforeseen disruptions. Road closures, vehicle breakdowns, travel delays, and demand fluctuations invalidate pre-established tactical plans and call for recovery decisions that simultaneously restore feasibility and limit deviation from the original schedule, two objectives inherently in tension under tight time constraints. We formulate recovery as a sequence of neighbourhood-restricted mixed-integer linear programs parameterised by a deviation bound $k$, the $\ell_1$ distance between the recovered and baseline plans. We show that the minimum feasible value $k_{\min}$ can be computed exactly, anchoring the search at its most stable extreme. Rather than targeting a single $k$, we seek a Pareto front of non-dominated recovery plans spanning the stability--cost space, giving dispatchers a structured set of operational options. To navigate this space efficiently, we propose a reinforcement learning policy, trained with the REINFORCE policy-gradient algorithm, that adaptively selects successive values of $k$ from solver feedback, concentrating computational effort in productive regions and terminating exploration when further improvement is unlikely. Evaluated on weekly instances derived from historical data of a Canadian forestry partner, under disruption scenarios covering all event categories considered, the approach recovers richer Pareto fronts with fewer solver calls than fixed-$k$ grid and dichotomic search baselines, and produces feasible recovery plans within operationally acceptable reoptimization times across all configurations and disruption types.

</details>


### [243] [Boundary control and periodic trajectories of semilinear Euler equations with application to hydrogen transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20948)
*Alexander Zuyev,Peter Benner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了以入口压力和出口质量流率为边界的管道气体流动数学模型（等温欧拉方程），通过提升算子转化为非线性抽象微分方程，并推导了傅里叶系数表示。在抽象空间中建立了具有连续可微控制的非线性系统周期解的存在条件，并将其应用于实际氢气运输模型，评估供需周期性波动下的周期性运行状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究管道气体流动在周期性边界条件（供需波动）下的动态行为，建立严格的数学框架来分析周期解的存在性，为实际氢气运输系统的运行优化提供理论依据。
