Method: 采用等温欧拉方程描述管道气体流动，以入口压力和出口质量流率为边界控制；利用提升算子将偏微分方程转化为抽象空间中的非线性常微分方程；通过傅里叶级数展开获得解析表示；建立周期解存在性的泛函分析条件；最后应用于氢气运输模型进行数值验证。

Result: 成功推导了非线性抽象微分方程的傅里叶系数表示，建立了周期解存在性的充分条件，并在氢气运输模型中验证了供需周期性波动下周期性运行状态的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的数学框架能够有效分析和预测管道气体流动在周期性边界条件下的运行状态，对氢能基础设施的调度和优化具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: A mathematical model of gas flow in a pipeline controlled by the inlet pressure and the outlet mass flow flux is considered in the form of the isothermal Euler equations with an appropriate equation of state. Within the framework of boundary control systems, this model is transformed into a nonlinear abstract differential equation using an appropriate lifting operator. For this abstract equation, a representation in terms of Fourier coefficients is derived analytically. Conditions for the existence of periodic solutions to a broad class of nonlinear control systems with continuously differentiable controls are established in abstract spaces. This framework is applied to a realistic hydrogen transport model to evaluate periodic operating regimes under periodic fluctuations in supply and demand.

</details>


### [244] [Dynamic Cloud Service-Capacity Deployment with Costly Response Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20961)
*Ruihan Zhou,Xiaowei Zhang,Yijie Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI产品发布时，在缺乏历史工作负载数据的情况下，如何联合决策当前容量部署与未来响应就绪状态。通过构建有限时域动态规划模型，提出冷启动信念可行动策略（CBAP），利用预测信念生成工作负载场景来估计决策价值，从而避免过早部署和不必要的就绪成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI产品发布后，服务容量决策需要在缺乏足够产品特定工作负载历史的情况下做出。早期服务观测虽能更新对剩余工作负载的信念，但工作负载不像库存需求那样物理消耗容量。同时，保持快速响应能力可能需要昂贵的容量接入和运营安排。因此，企业需要研究如何联合决策当前容量部署与未来响应就绪状态。

Method: 构建有限时域动态规划模型，包含工作负载信念、可服务容量和吸收性响应通道状态。提出保存-部署分解方法，将问题分解为每个延续选择的部署子问题并比较优化后的值。基于此结构开发冷启动信念可行动策略（CBAP），利用预测信念生成的工作负载场景估计各侧价值，并建立决策与生命周期性能损失的理论界限。

Result: 数值实验和基于BurstGPT的评估表明，CBAP相比基准策略能降低生命周期成本，避免过早部署和不必要的就绪支出。结果区分了预测信息性与运营可行动性，证明当前部署与未来响应就绪是分离的决策维度。零部署可能代表待命而非退出，而正部署不一定意味着需要保留响应通道。

Conclusion: 当前容量部署与未来响应就绪是企业需要分别考虑的独立决策变量。CBAP策略能有效平衡部署时机与就绪成本，在信息有限的环境下优于传统基准策略。研究为AI产品发布的容量管理提供了新的决策框架。

Abstract: AI product launches require service-capacity decisions before sufficient product-specific workload histories are available. Early service observations update beliefs about remaining workload, but workload does not physically deplete capacity as inventory demand does. Preserving rapid-response capability may also require costly capacity-access and operational arrangements. We study how a firm should jointly decide current capacity deployment and future response readiness. We formulate a finite-horizon dynamic program with workload beliefs, serviceable capacity, and an absorbing response-channel state. A preservation-deployment decomposition solves one deployment problem for each continuation choice and compares the resulting optimized values. This structure identifies when response readiness should be preserved and how much capacity should be deployed. We operationalize the framework through the Cold-Start Belief Actionability Policy (CBAP), which estimates side-specific values using workload scenarios drawn from predictive beliefs. We also establish bounds on its decision and lifecycle performance losses. Numerical experiments and a BurstGPT-based evaluation show that CBAP reduces lifecycle cost relative to benchmark policies by avoiding premature deployment and unnecessary readiness spending. The results distinguish forecast informativeness from operational actionability and show that current deployment and future response readiness are separate decision margins. Zero deployment may represent Standby rather than Exit, while positive deployment need not imply preserving the response channel.

</details>


### [245] [Control Co-design of systems with parabolic PDE dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20975)
*Antika Yadav,Prasad Vilas Chanekar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有抛物线型偏微分方程(PDE)动力学的系统的控制协同设计(CCD)综合问题，通过推导充分稳定性条件、空间离散化得到可计算的近似CCD问题，并采用梯度方法求解，最后通过实例验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有抛物线型PDE动力学系统的控制与设计协同优化问题，这类系统在传热、扩散等工程领域中广泛存在，而传统的分离式设计方法往往无法获得全局最优解。

Method: 首先推导了PDE的充分稳定性条件；然后对PDE进行空间离散化，结合充分稳定性条件构造了一个计算上易于处理的近似CCD问题；最后采用基于梯度的方法对近似问题进行求解。

Result: 通过一个实例验证了所提出的CCD综合方法的有效性，表明该方法能够有效解决抛物线型PDE系统的控制协同设计问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于充分稳定性条件和空间离散化的CCD方法能够为抛物线型PDE系统提供一种计算可行的协同设计框架，梯度求解方法在实例中表现良好。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the control co-design (CCD) synthesis problem for a class of systems with parabolic partial differential equation (PDE) dynamics. We first derive a sufficient stability condition for the PDE. By spatially discretizing the PDE and using the sufficient stability condition, we propose a computationally tractable approximate CCD problem. We solve the approximate CCD problem using a gradient-based method. Finally, we justify our proposed approach through an example.

</details>


### [246] [Stabilization of 1D Linear Hyperbolic Balance Laws by Integral Difference Control and Application to Networks Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21060)
*Adam Braun,Jean Auriol,Lucas Brivadis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的指数镇定方法，适用于一阶线性双曲平衡律在一般驱动（包括欠驱动边界控制和域内控制）下的问题。通过切割与折叠变换，该方法进一步扩展到含回路的双曲平衡律网络。控制设计基于可逆反步变换，将系统部分解耦，并在积分差分方程层面构造动态反馈增益，结合稳定的秩降过程和Corona问题的插值方程求解。数值仿真验证了该方法在现有技术无法处理的回路网络中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅适用于特定驱动配置（如特定边界或域内控制），缺乏统一框架来处理一般驱动（包括欠驱动边界和域内控制）以及含回路的网络化双曲平衡律系统的指数镇定问题。

Method: 1) 利用切割与折叠变换将网络化系统统一建模；2) 设计可逆反步变换部分解耦系统；3) 将镇定问题重构为积分差分方程层面的问题；4) 采用稳定的秩降过程将问题简化为单输入设计；5) 求解由Corona问题导出的插值方程来构造动态反馈律增益。

Result: 成功建立了统一框架，能够处理一般驱动配置下的指数镇定问题，包括现有方法无法解决的含回路网络系统，并通过数值仿真验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该统一方法显著扩展了双曲平衡律镇定问题的适用范围，为更复杂的网络化系统（特别是含回路的配置）提供了可行的控制设计途径。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified method for the exponential stabilization of first-order linear hyperbolic balance laws under general actuation, including both underactuated boundary and in-domain control. The proposed framework brings together a wide range of underactuated configurations within a single formulation and substantially extends existing results restricted to particular actuation settings. Using cutting and folding transformations, the proposed approach is further applied to networks of hyperbolic balance laws, including configurations with cycles. The control design is developed under a stabilizability condition and a robustness assumption. It is based on an invertible backstepping transformation, which partially decouples the system, followed by a reformulation of the stabilization problem at the level of an Integral Difference Equation (IDE). The gains of the resulting dynamic feedback law are constructed at the IDE level by combining a stable rank-reduction procedure, which reduces the problem to a single-input design, with the solution of an interpolation equation arising from a Corona problem. Numerical simulations are presented for a relevant cycle network that cannot be addressed by existing methods in the literature.

</details>


### [247] [Exact ensemble controllability for neural differential equations via neural interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21112)
*Martin Gugat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由神经动力学（深度神经网络）控制的系统，分析了系统的精确集合可控性，即用单一控制参数将N个不同初始状态引导至对应的N个目标状态的能力。通过将问题转化为神经插值问题，证明了若微分方程右端由两层神经网络给出，则插值问题可简化为求解线性方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确集合可控性在机器学习中至关重要，它关乎单个神经动力学系统能否同时学习执行不同任务，即用同一组控制参数将多个初始状态分别引导至对应的目标状态。

Method: 将精确集合可控性问题转化为神经插值问题，通过构造性方法求解；当右端函数由两层神经网络给出时，将插值问题简化为线性方程组的求解。

Result: 给出了精确集合可控性问题的构造性解决方案，并证明在两层神经网络条件下，对应的插值问题可转化为线性方程组求解。

Conclusion: 该研究为神经动力学的精确集合可控性提供了理论保证和构造性方法，对多任务学习等机器学习应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: We study a system that is governed by neural dynamics. Neural dynamics are a model for deep neural networks with a large number of layers. For a differential equation where the right-hand side is given by a neural network, we analyze the exact ensemble controllability of the system. This property plays an essential role in Machine Learning. It concerns the ability to learn to perform different tasks simultaneously with a single neural dynamics: Exact ensemble controllability requires that $N$ different initial states are steered to corresponding $N$ target states with a single set of control parameters.
  We present a constructive solution to the problem. The construction is based on the solution of a neural interpolation problem.
  We show that if the right-hand side of the differential equation is given by a neural network of depth two, the interpolation problem can be reduced to the solution of a system of linear equations.

</details>


### [248] [A Gaussian smoothing-based zeroth-order method for Goldstein second-order stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21258)
*Ming Lei,Ting Kei Pong,Man-Chung Yue,Liaoyuan Zeng,Hao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的广义Hessian矩阵（Goldstein二阶δ-次微分）及相应的(ε₁,ε₂,δ)-二阶平稳点概念，并设计了一种基于三次正则化和高斯平滑的零阶算法，用于在Lipschitz可微函数中寻找此类近似二阶平稳点，同时在目标函数满足温和的强制型假设下推导了迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理非光滑或仅有零阶信息（梯度不可直接获取）的优化问题时，难以有效利用二阶信息加速收敛。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一种可在仅有函数值信息（零阶）下利用广义二阶导数信息的理论框架和高效算法。

Method: 1）提出了Goldstein二阶δ-次微分，作为经典次微分的二阶推广；2）定义了(ε₁,ε₂,δ)-二阶平稳点的概念，放宽了严格二阶平稳性的要求；3）设计了基于三次正则化（cubic regularization）和高斯平滑（Gaussian smoothing）的零阶算法，并结合同伦策略（homotopy）逐步逼近；4）在目标函数满足强制型（coercivity）假设下进行复杂度分析。

Result: 成功推导了所提零阶算法在温和强制型假设下的迭代复杂度上界，表明算法能够以可证明的收敛速率找到近似二阶平稳点。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和算法两个层面拓展了零阶优化中二阶信息的应用，为仅能访问函数值的非线性优化问题提供了新的二阶收敛保证。

Abstract: We introduce a new generalized Hessian, called the Goldstein second-order $δ$-subdifferential, and an associated notion of $(ε_1,ε_2,δ)$-second-order stationary point for continuously differentiable functions with locally Lipschitz gradients. We propose a zeroth-order algorithm based on cubic regularization and Gaussian smoothing with homotopy to find such approximate second-order stationary points for Lipschitz differentiable functions, and derive the iteration complexity under a mild coercivity-type assumption on the objective function.

</details>


### [249] [Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov inclusions of the mean field type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21297)
*Yurii Averboukh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Wasserstein空间中由带有约束系数的Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov方程驱动的动力系统，在凸值、上半连续等假设下证明了解的存在性和解集的紧性，并研究了最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Wasserstein空间中由受约束的Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov方程驱动的动力系统，该系统的系数依赖于当前点和当前测度，是一个非平凡的无穷维动力系统问题。

Method: 假设约束映射是凸值的、上半连续的且满足增长条件，利用泛函分析和无穷维动力系统理论建立存在性定理和紧性结果。

Result: 证明了在给定假设下解的存在性，并建立了解集的紧性。

Conclusion: 在理论存在性和紧性基础上，进一步研究了此类动力系统的最优控制问题。

Abstract: The paper studies a dynamical system in the Wasserstein space, where the evolution is governed by a Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov equation with coefficients chosen from a prescribed set that depends at each point on that point and on the current measure. Under the assumption that the multivalued mapping determining the constraints is convex-valued, upper semicontinuous and satisfies certain growth conditions, we prove an existence theorem and establish the compactness of the solution set. Moreover, we study an optimal control problem for such dynamical systems.

</details>


### [250] [Decomposing a Multi-Scale Optimization Framework for Grid-Integrated Electrolysis using Aggregate-Informed Benders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21440)
*Kiernan X. Jennings,Victor M. Zavala,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚合信息的Benders分解方法，用于解决电解制氢系统在需求响应操作中的大规模多尺度优化问题，在高频电力市场参与和长时间跨度场景下实现了高效求解，相较于传统Benders分解方法可将最优性差距降低约81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电解制氢系统的需求响应优化模型在扩展到参与高频电力市场（如实时市场）和/或更长的时间跨度时，面临计算可扩展性差的问题，传统方法难以高效求解。

Method: 提出了聚合信息增强的Benders分解方法（aggregate-informed Benders decomposition），通过整合子问题的聚合信息加速收敛，求解了涵盖日前市场（DAM）和实时市场（RTM）的40年时间跨度的电解系统优化问题。

Result: 在40年时间跨度的实例中，传统Benders分解停滞在约85%的最优性差距，而所提方法将最终最优性差距降低了约81%；同时发现参与实时市场因更高的价格波动性而具有经济优势。

Conclusion: 聚合信息增强的Benders分解方法能有效解决电解系统多尺度优化在大规模实例下的计算可扩展性问题，为考虑设备寿命衰减的高频市场参与和长周期规划提供了可行的求解途径。

Abstract: Demand response (DR) operation of electrolysis devices is gaining traction to capitalize on volatile electricity markets, but their dynamic operation poses challenges to the durability and lifespan of these systems. Our recently developed multi-scale optimization framework for grid-integrated electrolysis systems studied the impacts of DR and effects on device durability. A major hurdle in that work is tractably scaling the model to include participation in high-frequency electricity markets and/or longer time horizons. To address this, in this work, we developed an aggregate-informed Benders decomposition method to tractably solve large instances of this problem structure. To illustrate the efficacy, we solve a case study with market participation in the day-ahead market (DAM) and real-time markets (RTM) for up to 40 years to effectively capture the effects of device lifespan change. We compare our decomposition algorithm to both commercial solvers and traditional Benders decomposition. We find that incorporating aggregate subproblem information accelerates convergence, reducing the final optimality gap by up to ~81% relative to traditional Benders on the 40-year horizon instances that otherwise stall near 85%. We additionally apply the algorithm to find that RTM participation offers economic advantages under the higher price volatility.

</details>


### [251] [Climate-resilient electric vehicle charging infrastructure for sustainable cities: An interpretable causal-ensemble framework for preventive maintenance and low-carbon mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21444)
*Cande Lian,Wentao Zeng,Jiabin Wu,Yiming Bie,Wei Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出FGDSE，一种特征引导的动态堆叠集成方法，用于预测电动汽车充电桩在未来1-30天内的故障风险。该方法将异构信号分为四类特征族，分配不同领域专家模型，并结合门控机制和因果推断（SHAP、X-learner），在25个月的13个站点数据上超越12个基线模型，30天时宏观召回率约85%，AUC仅下降3.2点。研究发现故障主导因素从历史故障转向气候压力，极端高温是唯一因果效应随时间增强的暴露因素，约30%站点为热敏感型，为气候适应性维护提供了量化阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市气候压力（极端高温、强降水、湿度）日益增加电动汽车充电桩的设备故障风险，而传统的事后维修模式效率低下，亟需向预防性维护转变。然而，多源异构信号（物理、行为、上下文、历史）在不同时间尺度上的复杂性，以及需要预测长达30天的故障风险，使得现有方法难以胜任。

Method: 提出FGDSE框架：1) 将异构信号分为四个特征族（物理、行为、上下文、历史），分别匹配领域专家模型；2) 引入两个深度时序专家分别捕捉短期脉冲和长期退化；3) 设计时域门控机制学习自适应权重，实现1-30天日级故障风险预测；4) 结合SHAP归因和X-learner将概率输出扩展为因果决策支持，提供后处理层面的处理效应。

Result: 在25个月13个站点的数据上，FGDSE在10天以上预测窗口超越12个基线模型；30天时宏观召回率保持约85%，AUC仅下降3.2点；揭示故障主导因素从历史故障转向气候压力；极端高温是唯一因果效应随时间增强的暴露因素，约30%站点被标记为热敏感型。

Conclusion: FGDSE为气候韧性充电资产管理提供了可解释的决策支持系统，通过量化气候适应性维护的阈值，有助于增强城市交通韧性和支撑低碳出行。

Abstract: Reliable electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure is a cornerstone of sustainable, low-carbon cities, yet urban climate stress such as extreme heat, heavy precipitation, and humidity increasingly raises equipment fault risk and undermines the resilience of urban energy and mobility services. Shifting operation from reactive repair to preventive maintenance depends on accurate, forward-looking fault-risk prediction, a task complicated by the heterogeneous time scales of physical, behavioral, contextual, and historical signals and by forecasting over a multi-week horizon. We develop FGDSE, a feature-governed dynamic stacking ensemble that forms an interpretable decision-support system for climate-resilient charging-asset management. It partitions heterogeneous signals into four feature families, assigns each to a domain expert whose inductive bias matches the data, and adds two deep temporal experts for short-term pulses and long-term degradation; a horizon-wise gating mechanism then learns adaptive weights to forecast daily fault risk over 1 to 30 days. SHAP attribution and an X-learner extend the probabilistic output into causal decision support with post-level treatment effects. On 25 months of data from 13 stations, FGDSE surpasses twelve baselines beyond the ten-day horizon, sustains about 85% macro-recall at 30 days with an AUC decay of only 3.2 points, and reveals a shift of dominance from fault history toward climate stress. It identifies extreme heat as the sole exposure whose causal effect amplifies over time, flagging roughly 30% of posts as heat-sensitive and yielding quantitative thresholds for climate-adaptive maintenance that strengthens urban mobility resilience and sustains low-carbon travel.

</details>


### [252] [Asymptotic Analysis of Empirical Dynamic Programming in Infinite-Horizon Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21520)
*Xin Chen,Elif Sena Isik,Johannes Milz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了离散时间无限水平折扣随机最优控制问题中基于样本近似的统计极限定理，包括唯一最优策略下的函数中心极限定理（极限为均值零高斯过程）和非唯一最优策略下的极限定理（可能非高斯），并比较了与样本策略优化的渐近差异，通过库存控制和可再生资源开采验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于样本的随机最优控制近似方法提供严格的统计理论基础，刻画其渐近分布性质，并揭示最优策略唯一性对极限分布的影响。

Method: 推导函数中心极限定理，通过线性不动点方程刻画高斯极限过程；对比样本值函数与样本策略优化的渐近方差；对非唯一最优策略情形推导可能非高斯的极限分布；通过库存控制和可再生资源开采进行数值验证。

Result: 在唯一最优策略条件下，样本值函数的极限为均值零高斯过程，由类似动态规划原理的线性不动点方程刻画；样本值函数与样本策略优化的极限方差不同；非唯一最优策略下极限分布可能非高斯。

Conclusion: 建立了离散时间无限水平折扣随机最优控制中样本近似方法的渐近理论，揭示了最优策略唯一性对极限分布性质的关键影响。

Abstract: We derive statistical limit theorems for sample-based approximations of infinite-horizon discounted stochastic optimal control problems in discrete time. Our first result is a functional central limit theorem for the sample-based value function under a uniqueness-type condition on population optimal policies. The limiting law is a mean-zero Gaussian process characterized by a linear fixed point equation that resembles a dynamic programming principle. We compare these asymptotics with those obtained from sample-based policy optimization and illustrate that their limiting variances can be different. We also derive a limit theorem for models with nonunique optimal policies, where the limiting law may be non-Gaussian. Applications to inventory control and renewable harvesting illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [253] [Barzilai-Borwein Fails Superlinear Convergence on an Open Set of Quadratics for Every Dimension $n\geq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21579)
*Dawei Li,Xiaotian Jiang,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了Barzilai-Borwein (BB1)方法在严格凸二次规划问题中，对于n≥4维空间存在一个非空开集（正Lebesgue测度）的问题和初始点，使得BB1方法收敛但无法达到根超线性收敛速度，梯度范数和误差能量范数被几何序列双向界定，排除了超线性收敛的可能性。该证明基于四维投影化BB动力学的非共振吸引七周期的计算机辅助证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BB方法在实际连续优化中表现出色，但其收敛动力学机制尚不明确，特别是BB方法是否对几乎所有严格凸二次问题和初始化都能达到超线性收敛这一核心问题尚未解决。

Method: 通过计算机辅助证明，在四维空间中构造了一个非共振的吸引七周期，并将其投影化到BB动力学中，从而构造出n≥4维严格凸二次问题及初始点的非空开集族。

Result: 对于n≥4维，存在一个非空开（正Lebesgue测度）的严格凸二次问题及初始点族，使得长步BB1方法收敛但无法根超线性收敛。梯度各谱分量被几何序列ρ_min=10^{-6}和ρ_max=0.61界定，梯度范数和能量范数满足双向几何估计，目标间隙满足平方率的几何估计，所有三个量都被几何序列下界限制。

Conclusion: 否定了BB方法在严格凸二次问题中几乎处处超线性收敛的猜想，揭示了BB方法在特定条件下只能达到几何收敛速率。

Abstract: Barzilai--Borwein (BB) method has shown strong practical performance in continuous optimization, yet its convergence dynamics remains poorly understood. In particular, a central unresolved question is whether BB converges superlinearly for almost every strictly convex quadratic problem and initialization. We provide a negative answer to this question. Specifically, for every finite dimension $n\geq4$, we construct a nonempty open, hence positive-Lebesgue-measure, family of strictly convex quadratic problems and initial points for which the long Barzilai--Borwein method (BB1) converges but cannot converge root-superlinearly. More precisely, with the explicit constants $ρ_{\min}=10^{-6},ρ_{\max}=0.61$, every spectral component of the gradient is bounded above and below by the corresponding geometric sequence. Consequently, the gradient norm and the energy norm of the error satisfy two-sided geometric estimates with the same rates, while the objective gap satisfies the corresponding estimates with squared rates. In particular, all three quantities are bounded below by geometric sequences, ruling out superlinear convergence. The construction is highly nontrivial, based on a computer-assisted proof of a nonresonant, attracting seven-cycle of the projectivized BB dynamics in dimension four.

</details>

### [254] [SPECTRA: State-Space Exogenous Context and Temporal-Frequency Resolution Architecture for Probabilistic Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20587)
*Hang Ye,Xinyan Jiang,Yuedong Shi,Yangxin Zhu,Jianming Wei,Tian Zheng,Xiaoying Zheng,Yongxin Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种状态空间外生上下文与时频分辨率架构，通过自适应分离确定性与随机性流、对齐外生变量、多分辨率谱时状态空间建模及有序分位数边界估计，实现通用概率性能源预测。在负荷、电价、光伏和风电四个预测任务中，18个设置下14个取得最优CRPS，平均降低5.74%，上尾分位数风险降低7.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多尺度分解、外生变量对齐和概率输出视为独立步骤，未能阐明可预测结构与不确定性波动如何共同塑造预测分布。本文旨在通过确定性与随机性分离的设计原则，统一处理这些方面，提升概率预测性能。

Method: 提出状态空间外生上下文与时频分辨率架构：(1) 自适应分离趋势周期成分（确定流）与高频残差（随机流）；(2) 将外生上下文与两流对齐；(3) 通过多分辨率谱时状态空间模型精化确定性主干；(4) 从互补表征中估计有序分位数边界，输出概率预测。

Result: 在负荷、电价、光伏和风电四个任务共18个设置中，14个取得最佳CRPS，平均CRPS降低5.74%，上尾分位数风险降低7.27%。

Conclusion: 确定性与随机性分离是通用概率性能源预测的有效设计原则，该架构在多种能源预测任务中均优于现有最强基线。

Abstract: Modern power systems increasingly require probabilistic forecasts amid interacting uncertainties from renewable intermittency, flexible demand, market volatility, and weather-dependent generation. However, existing methods often treat multi-scale decomposition, exogenous-variable alignment, and probabilistic output as separate steps, obscuring how predictable structures and uncertainty-bearing fluctuations jointly shape the forecast distribution. This paper proposes a state-space exogenous-context and temporal-frequency resolution architecture for general probabilistic energy forecasting. Its central premise is that trend-periodic components primarily determine the baseline trajectory, whereas high-frequency residuals and external perturbations govern the spread and asymmetry of forecast uncertainty. Accordingly, the architecture adaptively separates deterministic and residual streams, aligns exogenous context with both, refines the deterministic backbone through multi-resolution spectral-temporal state-space modeling, and estimates ordered quantile boundaries from their complementary representations. Experiments on load, price, solar, and wind forecasting achieve the best continuous ranked probability score in 14 of 18 settings, reducing average CRPS by 5.74\% and upper-tail quantile risk by 7.27\% over the strongest baselines. These results support deterministic-stochastic separation as an effective design principle for general probabilistic energy forecasting.

</details>


### [255] [Automatic knot selection in smooth additive models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21083)
*Nicolás Carrizosa,Vanesa Guerrero,María Durbán*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应样条(A-splines)扩展的新型显式结选择技术，结合定制的Fellner-Schall调参方案，用于广义可加模型(GAMs)。实验表明该方法在保持与P-splines等主流方法相当性能的同时，能构建出使用更少基元素数量的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统B样条回归中，节点(knots)的数量和位置选择对模型性能至关重要。尽管P-splines等正则化方法已成为GAMs的标准手段，但显式节点选择技术因计算或建模限制常被忽视，而后者在某些场景下具有独特优势。本文旨在开发一种显式节点选择方法以弥补这一空白。

Method: 基于自适应样条(A-splines)的节点选择方法论进行扩展，结合定制的Fellner-Schall方案来调节相关参数，实现GAMs中节点位置的显式选择。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和真实数据集上的评估表明，该方法与P-splines及前沿节点选择技术相比性能相当，但构建的模型使用的基元素数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 提出的显式节点选择技术能够在保持模型拟合性能的同时大幅降低模型复杂度，在某些应用场景下相比正则化方法具有实用价值。

Abstract: B-spline regression constitutes a widely used framework for nonparametric modeling. The performance of this methodology depends on specifying the number and placement of changepoints, known as knots, prior to the estimation process. Such knot sequence determines the dimension of the B-spline basis used to represent the regression function and the number of coefficients to be estimated. Therefore, the knots' choice affects the model's flexibility, influencing its smoothness and goodness-of-fit. Traditionally, this problem has been addressed either by explicitly selecting knots, via knot-selection algorithms, or by regularization methods, such as P-splines, which automatically tune the regressor's smoothness. The latter have become the standard in generalized additive models (GAMs). In contrast, knot-selection techniques, frequently neglected because of computational or modeling limitations, provide certain advantages which can be valuable in some contexts. In this work, we introduce a novel explicit knot-selection technique for GAMs based on an extension of the adaptive splines (A-splines) knot selection methodology, combined with a customized Fellner-Schall scheme for tuning the associated parameters. Our approach is evaluated on various synthetic and real datasets and compared with P-splines and state-of-the-art knot-selection techniques. The results indicate comparable performance, while producing models built on a substantially smaller number of basis elements.

</details>


### [256] [Transformer-based Diffusion models for Hydrological Time Series Probabilistic Imputation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21200)
*Ferdinand Bhavsar,Lionel Benoit,Maxime Savatier,Edith Gabriel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的扩散模型，用于在观测数据有限的情况下对水文气象时间序列进行建模和重建。研究将该框架应用于法国东北部六个地点的水量和水质联合建模，并与多个基线方法比较。结果表明，该方法在时间序列插补和预测方面均能有效捕捉和模拟水文数据的复杂模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文气象时间序列由于过程的高度变异性和观测数据的稀疏性，传统统计方法难以准确表示其动态特征，亟需更有效的建模方法。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的扩散模型框架，用于水文时间序列的模拟、重建（插补）和预测，联合建模水量和水质数据，在六个站点上进行校准和验证。

Result: 所提出的Transformer扩散模型在再现观测信号的关键时间特征方面优于多个基线方法，能够高效地从真实分布中采样，适用于存在变量缺失数据的预测和插补场景。

Conclusion: Transformer扩散模型能有效捕获和模拟水文数据中的复杂模式，在水文时间序列建模中具有显著潜力，尤其适用于观测数据有限的场景。

Abstract: The modeling of hydrometeorological time series with limited observations is a key challenge in the monitoring of hydro-systems and water resources, as well as for flood or drought risk assessment. Due to the high variability of the underlying processes and the sparsity of available measurements, traditional statistical approaches often struggle to accurately represent their dynamics. In this context, recent advances in deep learning offer a promising direction for improving the representation and generation of complex temporal processes sampled at several observation sites. This study investigates the application of transformer-based diffusion models to the simulation and reconstruction of hydrological time series. The proposed framework is applied to the joint modeling of water quantity and quality at six sites spread across three adjacent headwater catchments located in North-East France on a limestone plateau covered by forests and field crops. The model is calibrated and validated using available observational data, which has been quality controlled and corrected for sensor drift and malfunction through collaborative efforts by LNE metrology expertise and Andra monthly quality control over more than 15 years. Its performance is compared with several established baseline approaches commonly used for time series modeling. Quantitative evaluation metrics are employed to assess the ability of the proposed method to reproduce key temporal characteristics of the observed signals in two settings: the imputation of incomplete time series and the forecasting of upcoming hydrological conditions.  Results support the effectiveness of the transformer-based approach and highlight its capacity to capture and simulate the complex patterns present in hydrological data. In particular, the results indicate that diffusion models can efficiently sample realistic time series distributions under observation settings with variable missing data for both forecasting and imputation.

</details>
